### Batch 169 — Shareholder-letter salutations skip as blocks; page-line guard bounds the longest line, not the paragraph; 5 stale rows repaired (2026-11-01)
Closes the batch-167 COIN Q3 FY2025 greeting-wart follow-up and the NFLX/AXON salutation cohort. Shareholder letters open "Fellow shareholders," / "Dear Shareholders," after the date — a greeting, never highlights — and it was served three ways: as the HEADLINE (NFLX — the headline loop picks the first short line it reaches, so 8 quarters carried the salutation as their headline), as lede content (COIN Q3 FY2025's split "Fellow" / "Shareholders," form, SKYD mid-body — twice in Q1 FY2026), and as a leading-bullet continuation (AXON — the salutation follows the last bullet and the block appends it as a wrap). **fix** (`data/filings.py`): `_salutation_block_len` skips the salutation as a block at all four collection paths (headline loop, lede, leading-bullet block, Highlights sections). In the headline loop a salutation means the letter has no headline at all (NFLX: date / salutation / bullets), so the lede starts after it, like the dateline branch. The pattern is deliberately salutation-shaped: an optional single capitalized word between the opener and "shareholders" ("Fellow Affirm Shareholders,") and an optional trailing comma or period — and the line must END at the salutation. A prose sentence that happens to mention shareholders ("Shareholders approved the merger") never matches, and an inline salutation ("Dear Shareholders, This is my first letter ...", Instacart/Affirm's flattened form) never matches either: dropping that line would eat the prose with it, so those stay served. **fix 2:** the page-line guard's 1000-char bound is on the longest LINE in the first paragraph, not the paragraph — the section path joins a bullet list with "\n", so a 1385-char bullet list read as one paragraph although no line exceeds 350 chars. NFLX Q3 FY2024 exposed it: once headline-less (salutation no longer the headline), its 5-bullet list was discarded as a "page-line" and live went None. A single line over 1000 chars still trips the guard exactly as before (the batch-163 dateline-less page-line test still passes). **Live repair: 5 stored rows replaced** under the batch-157 gates plus a NEW stored-core-preservation gate (every stored paragraph except the date/salutation lines must survive in live — a repair that trades real highlights for a two-word greeting removal is a bad trade): NFLX 2026-09-30 (329 → 308, salutation headline → bullets), AXON 2024-12-31 (888 → 862), AXON 2025-12-31 (1028 → 1002), SKYD 2026-03-31 (2693 → 2672, both salutation lines out), COIN 2025-09-30 (355 → 812, split salutation + date → full letter prose under the "Third Quarter 2025" headline; batch 168's entry said this row was not repaired — it is now). The repair's live text is byte-identical to the blast's new extraction for all 5 rows where a blast file exists. The probe/blast/repair scripts are left untracked in `scripts/` (`batch169_probe.py`, `batch169_probe_nflx.py`, `batch169_probe_axon.py`, `batch169_blast.py`, `batch169_repair.py`) per the batch-105–110 precedent; the NFLX/AXON/COIN Q4 FY2025 exhibit HTML is cached under the goal's `hidden_files/blast169_html/`.

**Tests:** 6 new in `tests/data/test_filings_batch169.py` (salutation block shapes incl. split form + inline/prose negatives; NFLX end-to-end — salutation is not the headline; AXON end-to-end — salutation drops from the bullet block; split salutation drops from the bullet block; salutation drops inside a Highlights section; headlineless bullet list over 1000 chars is not a page-line). The regression is pinned by stashing the src change: all 4 end-to-end shapes fail on old code (NFLX headline "Fellow shareholders", AXON/section/bullet-block serve the salutation), and the page-line miniature returns the salutation headline on old code. Full suite 1244 green, `git diff --check` clean. **Blast-radius:** old-vs-new live extraction via `git stash` over 284 quarters across 36 filers (batch-160 cohort + SKYD + GEV + NFLX + AXON; HTML cached so both runs see byte-identical input) shows 19 diffs, all intended: AXON ×8, NFLX ×8, SKYD ×2 — salutation lines only, nothing else in any file. The other 265 quarters are byte-identical, including all GEV quarters (footnote guard untouched), all shareholder-letter, image-only, and Tesla quarters, and SHOP Q3 2024 (salvage intact). The guard refinement changes nothing in the cohort (new-vs-new2 blast is empty) — it only fires on NFLX Q3 FY2024, outside the blast's 8-quarter window. ### Batch 170 — "◦" sub-bullets are bullet starts in the leading-bullet block; 7 stale NFLX rows repaired (2026-11-01)
Closes the batch-169 carried follow-up in full. Netflix nests "◦" (white bullet, U+25E6) sub-bullets under "•" items in its shareholder letters, and the leading-bullet block truncated at the first one: a "◦" line was appended as a wrap continuation of the "•" item, ended with terminal punctuation, and the next line was another "◦" line — not a "•"/"-"/"*" start — so the item-close rule fired and the block broke, losing every bullet after the first sub-bullet (NFLX Q4 FY2024 live was 204 chars: one "•" item + one "◦" line; the stored row is 801 chars). **fix** (`data/filings.py`): `_LEADING_BULLET_STARTS = ("•", "-", "*", "◦")` — "◦" is a bullet start in the block's item-start check, its item-close next-line check, and `_current_leading_item_text` (so the open-quote check judges the current item from the last "◦" line, not across it). One shared tuple so the three sites cannot drift again. Distinct from "○" (white circle, U+25CB), which the lone-marker rule already covers. **Live repair: 7 stored rows replaced** under the batch-157 gates plus the batch-169 stored-core-preservation gate: NFLX 2024-12-31 (801 → 1494), 2025-03-31 (1134 → 1456), 2025-09-30 (929 → 1442), 2025-12-31 (636 → 1340), 2026-03-31 (1332 → 1606), 2026-06-30 (767 → 1644), 2026-09-30 (308 → 1714 — re-repairs batch 169's own row, which the new code made stale again by serving the full list). The repair's live text is byte-identical to the blast's new extraction for all 7. The probe/blast/repair scripts are left untracked in `scripts/` (`batch170_probe_nflx.py`, `batch170_blast.py`, `batch170_repair.py`) per the batch-105–110 precedent; exhibits are the batch-169 cache under the goal's `hidden_files/blast169_html/`.

**Tests:** 3 new in `tests/data/test_filings_batch170.py` (verbatim NFLX Q4 FY2024 head end-to-end — the whole list served, salutation still dropped; block-level "◦" collection + prose-close; current-item starts at a "◦" line). The regression is pinned by stashing the src change: all 3 fail on old code. Full suite 1247 green, `git diff --check` clean. **Blast-radius:** old-vs-new live extraction via `git stash` over 284 quarters across 36 filers (batch-160 cohort + SKYD + GEV + NFLX + AXON; HTML cached so both runs see byte-identical input) shows 8 diffs, all NFLX, and every diff is a pure append — the old text is a prefix of the new text, and every added line is a "•"/"◦" bullet or the "Our summary results and forecast for Q..." transition sentence. The other 276 quarters are byte-identical, including all GEV quarters (footnote guard untouched), all shareholder-letter, image-only, and Tesla quarters, and SHOP Q3 2024 (salvage intact). **Repair-gate fix:** the repair script's `norm` now also strips the space after "(" and before ")" — the stored rows were flattened with spaced footnote markers ("( Adolescence 1 )") while the current flattening attaches them ("(Adolescence1)"), so without this the preservation gate mistook a flattening artifact for dropped content (NFLX 2025-03-31 was the only row affected; same class as the existing letter-digit join). Live: home, /company/NFLX, and the Q4 FY2024 / Q1 FY2025 / Q3 FY2025 / Q4 FY2025 / Q3 FY2026 detail-data pages (6 checks) all 200; all 5 sampled repaired detail pages serve 0 "Fellow" with their full "◦" lists (5, 4, 3, 6, 2 sub-bullets). Data-only batch, so no screenshot gate applies. The dev server was restarted via the documented launcher (serve script, kill by PID, no pkill) to load the new code.

### Batch 171 — Stale "Q1" source labels repaired (14 rows); backfill now self-heals stale labels (2026-11-01)
Closes the batch-169/170 carried NFLX 2024-09-30 source-label follow-up — and its 13 siblings. A DB audit found 14 stored rows whose `press_release_source` says "Q1" for a Q3 (or Q2) quarter: PLUG/XOM/MET/UNH/HD/AMZN/NET/NFLX/SOFI/KO/ABBV/MS/PEP/BRK.B, all the oldest quarter of a seed window (12 at 2024-09-30, MS at 2025-09-30, XOM at 2026-06-30). **Cause:** pre-batch-52 fossils — the old labeler counted forward from Q1 within each fiscal-year group, so a window opening mid-cycle labeled its oldest 10-Q "Q1". The batch-52 count-back fix corrected the algorithm (already pinned by `test_assign_fy_quarter_noncalendar_partial_oldest_year`), but stored labels were never refreshed: the backfill only fills blank fields, so a wrong-but-present label stayed wrong forever. The proof is in the rows themselves: 8 of the 14 carry the correct quarter in their guidance/transcript sources ("8-K guidance, Q3 FY2024"), filled by a later backfill after the fix, while the press-release source kept the fossil. **fix** (`services/financials.py`): `_correct_stale_source_label` — corrects a stored label only on a two-against-one vote (stored quarter disagrees with the live quarter, live quarter equals the row's XBRL fiscal_period, fiscal years agree), so only the quarter moves, never the year; wired into `_enrich_press_release_highlights` so the backfill corrects the label while the period is already re-fetched. **Live repair: 14 stored labels replaced, highlights untouched** (PLUG, XOM Q1→Q2 FY2026, MET, UNH, HD Q1→Q3 FY2025, AMZN, NET, NFLX, SOFI, KO, ABBV, MS Q1→Q3 FY2025, PEP, BRK.B — all "Q1 FYyyyy" → "Q3 FYyyyy" except XOM). Each row passed a same-exhibit gate (live re-fetch returns highlights containing the stored text's first content paragraph — headline, prose, or bullet). The probe/diagnose/repair scripts are left untracked in `scripts/` (`batch171_probe_labels.py`, `batch171_diagnose.py`, `batch171_repair.py`) per the batch-105–110 precedent.

**Tests:** 4 new in `tests/services/test_financials_batch171.py` (fossil shapes incl. the guidance-label form; guards — quarters agree, FYs disagree, live quarter ≠ fiscal_period, unparseable/missing inputs; integration — a stale-label row with blank guidance enriches to the corrected label with highlights untouched; negative — no correction when live disagrees with the row). The tests fail on stashed old code (collection error: the helper does not exist). Full suite 1251 green, `git diff --check` clean. **Blast-radius:** none on extraction — the change touches only the service layer (label correction in the backfill loop); the extractor and the discovery labeling are untouched, so no old-vs-new extraction blast applies. The repair itself is the live verification: every row's label was re-derived from a live EDGAR discovery + exhibit re-fetch. Live: home, /company/NFLX, /company/AMZN, and the NFLX/PLUG/AMZN Q3 FY2024 + XOM Q2 FY2026 detail-data pages (6 checks) all 200; the sampled detail pages serve the corrected labels with zero stale "Q1" labels. Data-only label repair + a service-layer guard, so no screenshot gate applies. The dev server was restarted via the documented launcher (serve script, kill by PID, no pkill) to load the new code.

### Batch 172 — Business Wire datelines recognized; AMZN live extraction restored (2026-11-01)
Closes the batch-171 AMZN Q3 FY2024 live bullet drop in full. Live was 837 chars (headline + dateline + 5 of the 13 metric bullets) against 2117 stored: the Business Wire dateline "SEATTLE—(BUSINESS WIRE) October 31, 2024—Amazon.com, Inc. ..." has no "CITY, ST" comma, so `_DATELINE_LEAD_RE` never matched it — `_leading_bullet_block` returned None (first body line isn't a bullet) and the lede fallback spent 1 of its 6 budget lines on the dateline. **Cause chain:** the stored row is a Sep-29 seed-time artifact (the old bare `\bhighlights?\b` section regex matched the "Some other highlights since the company's last earnings announcement include that Amazon:" intro sentence and served the 12 business bullets); batch 102's regex tightening plus the leading-block financial priority changed the live path, and the dateline form was never a recognized shape. **fix** (`data/filings.py`): `_DATELINE_LEAD_RE` gains a Business Wire branch — caps city with an optional mixed-case state abbreviation, one or two dashes (or a spaced dash) around the literal "(BUSINESS WIRE)" tag, an optional "Month DD, YYYY" date, and the lead sentence; attested forms are AMZN ("SEATTLE—(BUSINESS WIRE) October 31, 2024—..."), FICO ("BOZEMAN, Mont.--(BUSINESS WIRE)--October 30, 2024--..."), Dollar General ("GOODLETTSVILLE, Tenn.--(BUSINESS WIRE)--...", no date), Palantir ("DENVER— (BUSINESS WIRE) — ..."), DoorDash ("SAN FRANCISCO—(BUSINESS WIRE)—..."). The tag is the anchor (prose never opens with caps city + dash + wire tag) and a bare tag with nothing after it is a credit line, not a dateline. Second fix in the same path: Q4 releases carry a bare "Fourth Quarter 2024" period label between the dateline and the bullets, which also broke the leading block (the lede already drops it via `_is_lede_table_line`/`_is_lede_period_header`) — `_leading_bullet_block` now skips period-header lines before any bullet is collected, reusing `_is_lede_period_header`. **Result:** AMZN Q3 FY2024 live is 837 → 2635 chars (headline + all 13 metric bullets, no dateline; the trailing CEO quote is the block's standard append-then-break continuation, pre-existing). All 10 AMZN quarters now serve the full metric block under the batch-102 financial priority — Q1 2024's old 3999-char capped business-section result (its bare "Highlights" heading at line 22) becomes the 13-bullet metric block, and the Q4s serve their 9–10 Q4 bullets, stopping at the "Full Year YYYY" label. Stored rows are untouched (the backfill only fills blanks), so the user-visible AMZN pages keep the business bullets; the fix governs future seeds and backfills of blank rows. A lede-loop dateline skip was tried and REVERTED in this batch: the blast showed the freed budget line is usually filled by a footnote definition ("1. Non-GAAP measure...", "1 Refer to the tables..."), a contact role line ("Chief Financial Officer"), a bare table heading ("… GAAP Revenue"), or a table data row (DASH) — the tail-guard gaps are the prerequisite, not this batch.

**Tests:** 6 new in `tests/data/test_filings_batch172.py` (all 5 attested wire forms match, 6 negatives reject, leading-block + end-to-end for the Q3 and Q4 shapes). 5 fail on stashed old code (the negatives-only test passes on old code too, as expected). Full suite 1257 green, `git diff --check` clean. **Blast-radius:** old-vs-new live extraction via `git stash` over 436 quarters across 35 tickers (AMZN/FICO/DG/PLTR/DASH + the prior-batch cohorts; HTML cached so both runs see byte-identical input) shows 10 diffs, all AMZN, all improvements; the other 34 tickers are byte-identical, including all HAL/GOOGL/NFLX/COIN/DUOL/ABNB shareholder-letter, deck, and table cohorts. 3 new-only files (DASH 2023-12-31, HAL 2026-09-30, MU 2023-11-30) are discovery flakiness — the old run logged NO EXHIBIT URL while SEC 503s were served from stale cache; their content matches old-code behavior shapes (DASH lede still carries its dateline; HAL's financial bullets carry the pre-existing dateline continuation). The probe/diagnose/blast scripts are left untracked in `scripts/` (`batch172_probe.py`, `batch172_amzn_all.py`, `batch172_blast.py`, `batch172_blast_target.py`) per the batch-105–110 precedent. **Live:** dev server restarted via the documented launcher (serve script, kill by PID, no pkill) to load the new code; home, /company/{AMZN, FICO, DG, PLTR, DASH, HAL, GOOGL, NFLX} all 200 (9 checks), plus /company/AMZN/detail and detail/data for Q3 FY2024 (200) serving the stored business bullets ("Prime Big Deal Days" present, no "BUSINESS WIRE" line). ### Batch 173 — Tail guards: footnote pointers, bare-numbered footnotes, role lines, legends, "Key Points:" (2026-11-01)
Closes the batch-172 prerequisite in full: the tail guards the lede-dateline skip needs. **The dateline skip itself is DEAD** (decided this batch, not merely deferred): a whole-line `_DATELINE_LEAD_RE` skip in the lede loop, blasted over the full 1,699-exhibit cache, produced 130 diffs — ALL of them lead-sentence removals, 0 bare datelines. A dateline sentence carries real narrative (AAPL's carries revenue $94.9B / EPS $0.97; 70 stored rows serve a dateline sentence as lede line 1 — DIS, SHOP, CSX, MCO, DELL). A bare-only skip would change 0 quarters in the corpus, and prefix-stripping was rejected (130 rows of churn for cosmetic gain, no budget freed). **Fix** (`data/filings.py`), at all three collection paths — lede loop, leading-bullet block, and Highlights sections (the section path matters: Walmart's legend only surfaced through its Third-Quarter Highlights section, which the lede-path tests never exercised): (1) `_FOOTNOTE_POINTER_RE` — `^\d{1,3}[.)]?\s+(See|Refer to)\b` — a footnote-definition opener, plus a parenthesized alternative `^\(+(See|Refer to)\b` for Carnival's "(See “Non-GAAP Financial Measures” and “Reconciliation of Forecasted Data”)" tail pointer. (2) `_is_bare_numbered_footnote` — terminated sentence, 500-char cap (raised 300→400→500 across the blast iterations as attested footnotes ran to 470 chars: Amex's participating-award footnote; two longer ones still sit above the cap — Moody's 556-char medium-term-EPS disclaimer, Amex's 671-char FX-adjusted footnote), capital after the number (keeps batch 164's "1 million shares are included in the diluted count." wrapped continuation out; the bullet-start guard is subsumed by the capital requirement). (3) `_is_bare_role_line` — 2–6 words, all role vocabulary, ≥1 anchor word (Officer/President/Chairman/Director/Founder/Head/Counsel/Secretary/Treasurer/Principal) — drops Moody's dangling "Chief Financial Officer" / "President and Chief Executive Officer" quote attributions. (4) `_LEDE_SYMBOL_LEGEND_RE` gains the dash form `^["“”'][A-Za-z]{1,5}["“”']\s*[-–—]\s+\S` (Walmart's "“cc” - constant currency"; also drops Biogen's "AMR = antibody mediated rejection; CLE = ...; SLE = ..." abbreviation legend from a section). (5) "Key Points:" heading skip in the lede loop (same class as the batch-156 Highlights skip) — Disney's quarters gain the real bullet after it. (6) The salvage-path sentence filter gains the pointer + bare-number branches (drops Affirm's "1 Information about Affirm's use of non-GAAP..." footnote sentence from page-flattened lines). Period-form term+verb definitions: 0 stored rows — NOT implemented (unattested).

**Tests:** 11 new in `tests/data/test_filings_batch173.py` (pointer regex incl. the parenthesized form, bare-number opener + guards incl. the batch-164 lowercase-quantity continuation, role lines, legend dash form, end-to-end WMT/MCO/DIS lede, section-path legend+role drop, WMT section end-to-end). Full suite 1268 green, `git diff --check` clean. **Blast-radius:** cache-only old-vs-new over 1,699 exhibits / 142 tickers (cache at `~/workspace/goals/tickerlens-app-build/hidden_files/blast_html` — /tmp is a 512MB tmpfs and the discovery blast died on disk-full at 505MB, so the cache moved to home disk; `scripts/batch173_cache_blast.py` re-runs in ~40s with no fetching): 33 diffs, all improvements, 1,666 byte-identical — CCL ×4 (pointer lines dropped), DASH ×3 (footnotes dropped; Deliveroo offer + Q2 Financial Performance totals gained), DIS ×4 ("Key Points:" → real bullet), MCO ×9 (role lines + pointers dropped; quotes/bullets gained), XOM ×10 (footnote runs dropped), AXP ×1, DDOG ×1, BIIB ×1 (abbreviation legend dropped from a section). **Live repair:** `scripts/batch173_repair.py` (44 targets: the 32 blast quarters + WMT ×6, CSX ×3, PCAR ×2, DASH 2024-12-31) re-fetches live and gates on source-label match, headline verbatim, line-level stored-core preservation (defect lines excluded — the batch-170 paragraph gate has no defect exclusion and tripped on DIS/MCO's own defect lines; ### Batch 175 — Trane Wingdings "l" bullets + bare-dollar units line; TT ×7 repaired (2026-11-01)
Closes the batch-174 TT ×7 follow-up in full. **Cause chain:** Trane renders its highlight bullets as a Wingdings "l" glyph (`<font face="Wingdings">l</font>`), which flattens to a lone "l" ("l Reported revenues of $5.4 billion ...") — `_BULLET_LEAD_RE` (•●–—/-) never matched it, so the bullets were invisible to every bullet guard. Worse, the summary table's units line ("$, millions except EPS Q3 2024 Q3 2023 ...") has no parens, so neither units regex matched it (`_TABLE_UNITS_RE` requires `(`, `_TABLE_WORD_UNITS_RE` requires `(in`), and the Highlights section collector ran past the units line and served the table's data rows ("Bookings $5,213 $4,946 5% 5%", "Net Revenues $5,441 ...", "GAAP Operating Income $819 ...") as highlights — 5 of the 7 quarters. The figure-dense EPS bullet ("l GAAP continuing EPS of $3.43; adjusted continuing EPS* of $3.37, up 21 percent", 2 $ figures in 78 chars) was also misflagged as a data row by `_is_lede_data_row`'s $-figures branch, because that predicate had no bullet exemption (unlike `_is_lede_table_line`). Seed-time Q1/Q2 headlines were truncated at "Results;" while live carries the full "Results and Raises ... Guidance" headline. **Fix** (`data/filings.py`): (1) `_BULLET_LEAD_RE` gains `l\s` — the space stays required, so a line starting with the letter "l" as a word ("lower revenue ...") never matches (no English word is a lone "l"); this also exempts "l" bullets in `_is_lede_table_line` and stops them counting as data rows in `_looks_like_financial_table`. (2) `_TABLE_UNITS_RE` gains a bare-dollar alternative `^\$\s*,\s*(millions|thousands|billions)\b` — a line starting "$," plus a scale word is a units header, never prose; one definition, all call sites (section collector, leading-bullet block, `_is_lede_table_line`, `_looks_like_table_fragment`) inherit it. (3) `_is_lede_data_row` exempts bullet-led lines at the top — a highlight bullet is narrative, never a table row, and the predicate itself must say so or direct callers (repair residue scans, `_is_lede_table_label`'s next-line check) misflag figure-dense bullets. **Repair** (`scripts/batch175_repair.py`, TT-only, same gates as batch 174 plus two extensions): stored table-residue lines are defect lines for the preservation gates (the extractor's own `_is_lede_table_line` — bullets never match it, so a dropped bullet still trips the gate), and a live headline is accepted when the stored headline is a strict prefix of it (the truncated seed headline). **7/7 repaired, 0 skipped** — TT 2024-09-30 (1154→797), 2025-03-31 (1132→804), 2025-06-30 (1135→782), 2025-09-30 (1200→843), 2025-12-31 (1224→1004), 2026-03-31 (1174→822), 2026-06-30 (1176→860); all verified in the DB. TT 2024-12-31 was probed and is live == stored (1034 chars) under the new extractor — correctly not a target. The probe/diagnose/blast scripts are left untracked in `scripts/` (`batch175_probe_tt.py`, `batch175_cache_blast.py`, `batch175_repair.py`, `batch175_pred_probe.py`) per the batch-105–110 precedent.

**Tests:** 5 new in `tests/data/test_filings_batch175.py` ("l" bullet match + word guards, bare-dollar units match + prose guards, `_is_lede_data_row` bullet exemption + real-row positives, `_is_lede_table_label` bullet next-line negative + genuine-label positive, Trane-shaped end-to-end — all 5 bullets served, units line and all 3 data rows absent). Full suite 1278 green, `git diff --check` clean. **Blast-radius:** cache-only old-vs-new over 1,699 exhibits / 142 tickers (`scripts/batch175_cache_blast.py`, same cache as batches 173–174, baseline at `/tmp/blast175c/old`): **4 diffs, 1,695 byte-identical** — TT is not in the cache, so the TT fix itself produces zero cache diffs; all 4 come from the `_is_lede_data_row` bullet exemption via `_is_lede_table_label` (a subtitle above a figure-dense bullet was misclassified as a table label because the bullet below was misread as a values row — fixing the predicate fixes the classification). Each was reviewed individually: AJG ×2 — the dash-led value rows ("– – 20 14 ...") are no longer data rows, so their row labels ("Workforce and lease termination", "Acquisition related adjustments") are collected as content instead of being skipped as labels — the lede still serves the summary table (pre-existing wart), but labels + values is more readable than bare value rows; AWK 2024-12-31 — the "CEO HARDWICK TO RETIRE IN MAY; GRIFFITH TO ASSUME CEO ROLE" subtitle is now served (half-fixes the batch-174 AWK subtitle item — the other subtitle, "AFFIRMS LONG-TERM GROWTH TARGETS", is still skipped as a heading fragment, correctly) at the cost of the last bullet under the 6-line lede budget ("Affirmed long-term EPS and dividend growth targets of 7-9%") — the CEO transition is headline-grade news that was entirely absent from the served highlights, so this is a net improvement; CARR 2026-09-30 — the "Signed LTAs valued at multiple billions of dollars with minimum volume commitments" subtitle is served at the cost of the last bullet ("Returned ~$600 million to shareowners ...") — a recorded wash: a title-grade line for the 6th bullet, the lede's 6-line budget working as designed. The exemption was kept whole (not narrowed to exclude dashes) because the dash-led lines were already treated as bullets by `_is_lede_table_line` and `_looks_like_financial_table` — the change aligns the third predicate with the other two, and the blast proves the effect is confined to these 4 exhibits. **Live:** home + /company/{TT, WMT} + /company/TT/detail/data for Q3 FY2024 and Q1 FY2026 + /api/search?q=TT all 200 (6/6); rendered TT Q3 FY2024 detail data serves the EPS bullet and contains no table row. ### Batch 177 — Lede heading / table-header lines; MCO + DIS carried items closed; 49 stored rows repaired (2026-11-02)
The batch-173/176 carried items — Moody's summary-table header line riding the lede and Disney's section headings riding the lede — turn out to be one defect class DB-wide. **Attested:** MCO Q4 2025 serves "FOURTH QUARTER AND FULL YEAR SUMMARY FINANCIALS" plus "MCO Diluted EPS MCO Adjusted Diluted EPS2" as lede lines 2–3 above the CEO quote (the header line in 6 stored rows); COF serves "Impact After-Tax Diluted EPS" (5 rows) below "The quarter included the following adjusting items:"; DIS Q3 2026 serves "Fiscal 2026 outlook:" above the outlook bullets and Q1 2026 serves "Financial Results for the Quarter:". A DB-wide probe (`scripts/batch177_probe.py`, broad detectors over all 2,454 stored highlight rows) flags 216 rows; re-probing against fresh extraction over the cached exhibits splits them into 75 live gaps (FRESH-CLEAN), 14 probe false positives (all-caps sub-headlines carrying a verb — "Q2 ORGANIC SALES AND ADJUSTED EPS EXCEED OUTLOOK", "COMPANY MAINTAINS FULL-YEAR REVENUE AND EPS GUIDANCE" — and GOOGL's "Sundar Pichai, CEO of Google and Alphabet, said:" quote attribution, all correctly kept), and 127 no-cache rows. **Fix** (`data/filings.py`): three lede-loop skips after the "Key Points:" skip — (1) `_is_lede_colon_heading`: a colon-ended list-intro / section heading (the "the following:" / "as follows:" idiom fires regardless of verbs, with a tail-verb guard so "the following metrics will improve" stays prose; otherwise a bare noun phrase ≤8 words with no verb at all; "said"/"says" vetoes quote attributions); (2) `_is_lede_table_header`: a figure-less, unterminated, capital-led metric-label line with a repeated label word or an Adjusted/After-Tax/Pre-Tax/Non-GAAP/GAAP qualifier, with a headline-verb veto so sub-headlines survive; (3) `_is_lede_heading_fragment` extended 4→8 words with a headline-verb + no-figures guard, so "FOURTH QUARTER AND FULL YEAR SUMMARY FINANCIALS" drops while "Q2 ORGANIC SALES AND ADJUSTED EPS EXCEED OUTLOOK" and "AMERICAN WATER REPORTS SOLID SECOND QUARTER RESULTS" stay. The same two skips apply to the winning Highlights section — but **post-selection only**: the first attempt dropped them in the per-section cleanup, which changed candidate financial density and flipped Akamai's competition (the Full-year section won over the lede's Revenue section, losing the Security/Delivery/Compute bullets in 3 Q4 rows); reverted and re-blasted clean. **Audit:** the genuine-predicate scan finds 273 stored rows carrying one of the three classes (158 colon-only, 99 caps, 16 table-header incl. overlaps) — the repair cohort this batch is the 75 blast-verified cached rows; the no-cache remainder is a later cohort.

### Batch 176 — Paren footnote false positives drop real prose; ANET/MRK/PLUG lines rescued; FIS ×1 repaired (2026-11-02)
The batch-174 audit remainder (34 rows / 37 lines) split on re-probe into stale data and live extractor gaps. **Cause chain:** the paren footnote shape (`^\([a-z0-9]+\)`, batch 97) is the loosest marker — it also matches a parenthesized abbreviation that a wrapped sentence breaks on. Three attested: Arista's bullet wraps mid-parenthetical ("... multi-planar leaf-spine design" / "(MRC) protocol, and segment routing over IPv6 (SRv6), aimed at maximizing AI cluster utilization and resilience."); Plug's bullet wraps on its abbreviation ("... improved by 25% quarter-over-quarter" / "(QoQ) and 46% year-over-year (YoY), driven by margin expansion ..."); Merck's sentence wraps on its abbreviation ("... compared to intravenous" / "(IV) KEYTRUDA. Merck also announced the initiation of waveLINE-010 ..."). The Highlights section collector dropped all three as footnote-definition blocks — fresh extraction of ANET served a bullet with its tail cut off while the stored row still had it, the regression proof. **Fix** (`data/filings.py`): new `_is_paren_prose_continuation`, wired into `_opens_footnote_def`'s paren branch only — a paren-led line whose post-marker text starts lowercase (a sentence resuming mid-flow) or whose first sentence is a single 2+ letter alphabetic word with a new sentence after it ("(IV) KEYTRUDA." — a bare term, not a definition) is prose, not a footnote definition. The previous line is deliberately NOT consulted: a genuine definition can follow an unterminated table row (Lilly's "(1) Tirzepatide is marketed ..." follows the revenue table's last row), so a prev-line test would keep real footnotes. The 2+ letter alpha bound is load-bearing: a decimal figure ("(210) (0.67)") splits on its decimal point and an abbreviation ("U.S. ...") splits after one letter, so neither fires — CEG's table fragments and a bare "(1)" marker stay footnote/table residue. **Audit:** re-running `scripts/batch174_audit.py` takes the remainder from 34 rows / 37 lines to **31 rows / 34 lines** (ANET, PLUG, MRK 2025-03-31 leave the cohort). **Repair** (`scripts/batch176_repair.py`, same gates as batch 174, 31 targets = the 31 remainder rows minus AFRM plus FIS): **1 repaired, 30 skipped** — FIS 2025-12-31 (984→1362, verified in the DB: the "(ii) sale of all of its equity interests in Worldpay ..." list item the old code dropped is now served). ### Batch 178 — Exposed table-row/title leak: predicates + 13 stored rows repaired (2026-11-02)
The batch-177 repair's exposed leak — freed lede budget letting the loop reach lines the old extraction never served — turns out to be one predicate family DB-wide. **Attested:** ARCC ×5 serves the 86-char recon row "Fair value of accruing debt and other income producing securities(5) $ 24,831 $ 23,643" (above the 80-char `$-figures` cap), the units line "(dollar amounts in millions, except per share data) Total Amount Per Share ...", "As of December 31," and the 10-word yields label; COF ×7 serves the exhibit title "Capital One ... Earnings" plus (Q2 2024) the Walmart recon row; EL 2025-03-31 serves "Organic Net Sales, Non-GAAP1", the spaced-decimal "Diluted Net Earnings Per Common Share $ .44 $ .91 (52) %" and the "Adjusted Diluted Net Earnings Per Common Share, Non-GAAP1,2" header. **Fix** (`data/filings.py`, 15 changes): (1) `$-figures` cap 80→120, narrowed band only (81–120 chars) with guards — no bare-year ending, no `$` figure followed by a scale word, no `$-to-$` range, no flattened-list bullet (a blunt raise broke 6 tests: COIN wrapped prose, Chubb bullets, the batch-160 guard); (2) spaced-decimal `$` counting (`\$\s?\.?[\d(]`) in the data-row regex and final-branch count; (3) new `_is_lede_title_line` (trailing heading AND NOT a headline verb) wired as a lede-loop skip — the verb veto keeps "Q2 ORGANIC SALES AND ADJUSTED EPS EXCEED OUTLOOK", "COMPANY MAINTAINS FULL-YEAR REVENUE AND EPS GUIDANCE", "AMERICAN WATER REPORTS SOLID SECOND QUARTER RESULTS"; (4) `_is_lede_asof_date` (year-less "As of <Month> <day>,"); (5) table-header qualifier/repeat comparison strips trailing footnote digits ("Non-GAAP1,2" → "non-gaap") + "Earnings" added to the metric regex; (6) `_TABLE_UNITS_RE` singular "dollar" (lookahead excludes "(dollar-cost averaging...)"); (7) colon-heading bound 8→12 words; (8) headline-verb list gains AFFIRMS/PROVIDES/CONFIRMS/REVISES/INITIATES/REITERATES/REITERATING (AWK/CPB/FDX/CHD sub-headlines); (9) `_LEDE_BARE_UNITS_RE` (Visa's "In billions, except percentages and per share data."); (10) glued footnote numbers ("1See pages 17 and 18..." — the `[A-Z][a-z]` lookahead keeps "3M reports..." out); (11) footnote-definition blocks cross blank lines and bare page numbers (ARCC's (7) yields footnote wraps across a page break: "... plus the net annual" / "2" / "" / "securities at amortized cost ..." — the "2" is a page number, an admin line and therefore a boundary); (12) lowercase bare-units form ("in millions, except per share data and percentages" — a sentence never opens lowercase); (13) "Dollars in millions, except EPS" form (DuPont); (14) `_clean_highlights_result` trims a trailing bare-units line (eBay's units fragment rides a Highlights section, so the lede-loop skip never sees it).

### Batch 179 — PR/IR Contact labels; batch-177 remainder repaired (28 stored rows) (2026-11-02)
The batch-177 remainder's CMG/AIG blocks turn out to be one extractor defect: Chipotle heads every release with a two-block contact head ("PR Contact: Laurie Schalow" / phone / email / "IR Contact: Cindy Olsen, CFA" / phone / email), and the contact regexes only knew the spelled-out roles (media/press/investor) — so the headline loop picked "PR Contact: Laurie Schalow" as the headline (it is headline-shaped) and every CMG stored row opens with it; AIG's stored rows open with the "www.aig.com Quentin McMillan (Investors): ..." contact lines, which the repair gates didn't recognise as contact-block lines. **Fix** (`data/filings.py`): `_CONTACT_LINE_RE` gains a word-boundaried `\b(pr|ir)[\s\-–]*(contacts?|inquiries?)\b` branch (the boundary keeps "their contact" / "first contact" out — the "ir" sits mid-word there), and `_CONTACT_HEADER_RE` gains `pr|ir` in its role prefix list, so the header branch fires and `_skip_contact_block` consumes the phone/email detail lines with it (traced: the block skip stops at the real headline, whose lookahead finds no contact line after it). **Tests:** 3 new in `tests/data/test_filings_batch179.py` (regex positives + prose negatives, verbatim CMG Q3 2024 head end-to-end). The pin is behavioral: on stashed old code the regex test and the end-to-end test fail. Full suite 1317 green, `git diff --check` clean. **Blast-radius:** cache-only old-vs-new over 1,699 exhibits / 142 tickers (`scripts/batch179_cache_blast.py`, baseline `/tmp/blast179c/old`, new `/tmp/blast179c/new`): **12 diffs, 1,687 byte-identical, 0 true content losses** — all CMG; 9 quarters drop the contact paragraph and gain one previously-starved bullet (freed lede budget: restaurant-level margin / diluted EPS), 3 quarters change only line 1 from the contact line to the real headline; 0 "Contact" strings remain in any new CMG output. **Repair** (`scripts/batch179_repair.py`, 29 targets — the 19-row remainder + all 8 CMG quarters + all 8 AIG quarters + 2 more wrapped-headline CPB rows, all blast-attested): **28 repaired, 1 skipped** — GEV ×8 (529→2442 … 527→1132, the stored rows were skeletons: live serves the cash bullets, CEO quote and segment prose the old extraction never reached), CMG ×7 (427→495 … 397→1553), AIG ×8 (726→887 … 575→828), CPB ×3 (417→644 … 750→1016, wrapped headlines joined), APD 2025-12-31 (527→726), CHD 2025-03-31 (276→330), all live==blast verified and DB-verified (a second `--write` run repairs 0/29, and a DB scan finds the only remaining contact-head is the skipped CMG 2025-06-30 row). The gates needed three rounds of their own: the lede-summary exclusion first matched any unterminated figure-free line before a colon heading — which included AIG's real headline — so it is now positional (the summary is the paragraph right after the first real paragraph); the wrapped-headline heuristic (fragment half + year in the join) falsely exempted CHD's "UPDATES FULL YEAR OUTLOOK" (its neighbour merely happened to be the year-carrying section title "2025 First Quarter Results"), so it is now an exact anchor (a stored paragraph is a headline half iff it joins with a neighbour into the live headline); and the "■" bullet marker is stripped in `_core_key` (AIG bullets are "■ " stored vs "■" live) while the residue scan skips "■"/"▪"/"○"-led lines as bullets (they were flagged as data rows). The one skip is a real content question, not gate noise: CMG 2025-06-30's all-caps sub-headline "HIGHLIGHTS RETURN TO POSITIVE COMPARABLE SALES AND TRANSACTIONS IN JUNE" is dropped by live and its June-positive-comps fact is not restated anywhere in the live text. **Live:** home + /company/{CMG, GEV, AIG, CPB, APD, CHD, ADBE} + 6 detail pages (CMG Q3 FY2024, GEV Q3 FY2024, AIG Q3 FY2024, CPB Q4 FY2025, APD Q1 FY2026, CHD Q1 FY2025) all 200 (14/14); the 6 detail pages serve the repaired headline/bullet with 0 contact residue. ### Batch 180 — "% change" footnote glued onto a quote attribution (Visa); 1 stored row repaired (2026-11-02)
Visa flattens its summary-table footnote ("% change is calculated over the comparable prior-year period."), the table's period header ("Q3 2024") and the CEO-quote attribution ("Ryan McInerney, Chief Executive Officer, Visa, commented on the results:") onto one lede line, served in 7 non-Q4 quarters — a batch-178 regression, since the freed lede budget (the bare-units line above it is now skipped) lets the loop reach it; the stored rows predate that and are clean, so live was WORSE than stored. **Fix** (`data/filings.py`, 3 changes): (1) new `_is_lede_footnote_attribution` — a line is table residue iff it carries BOTH the "% change is calculated" footnote fragment AND a quote-attribution tail (commented/said/noted/added + optional "on the results" + ":") — wired into `_is_lede_table_line` before the termination gate; both halves are required (a bare footnote line is a footnote-definition block; a bare attribution is a legitimate quote intro); dropping the line frees the budget for the quote itself, which follows the table; (2) `_is_lede_colon_heading`'s quote-attribution veto gains commented/comments — a bare "commented on the results:" attribution was classified as a colon heading and dropped, orphaning its quote ("noted"/"added" were already vetoed via `_LEDE_COLON_VERB_RE`); (3) the lede finish rule treats a quote attribution landing on the 6th budget line as unfinished (`_LEDE_QUOTE_ATTRIBUTION_TAIL_RE` in the finish-item decision + a break suppression), so the attribution still pulls its quote in. The broad version of (3) was tried and reverted: treating ANY colon-ended budget item as unfinished pulled Berkshire's table-label lines in (BRK.B ×7 "Net earnings includes:" + 27 extra diffs) — the attribution-specific form keeps the blast at 7 diffs. **Tests:** 5 new in `tests/data/test_filings_batch180.py` (attested V line, verb variants, bare-half negatives, colon-heading veto, Visa-style end-to-end with quote served, bare-attribution end-to-end). The pin is behavioral: on stashed old code the test file errors at collection (the predicate does not exist). Full suite 1322 green, `git diff --check` clean. **Blast-radius:** cache-only old-vs-new over 1,699 exhibits / 142 tickers (`scripts/batch175_cache_blast.py`, baseline `/tmp/blast180c/old`, final `/tmp/blast180c/new4`): **7 diffs, 1,692 byte-identical, 0 true content losses** — each V quarter swaps the defect line 1-for-1 for the CEO quote; 0 "% change is calculated" lines remain in any new output. **Repair** (`scripts/batch180_repair.py`, batch-179 gates, 8 V targets, all live==blast verified): **1 repaired, 7 skipped** — V 2024-06-30 (449→871: same 4 bullets + the CEO quote, minus the "Income Statement Summary" title line), DB-verified. ### Batch 181 — Emerson lede residue swap + FICO "GAAP Revenue" headings; 21 stored rows repaired (2026-11-02)
Batch 178 freed lede budget by dropping table residue, and the loop then reached deeper into Emerson's releases — where it served guidance-table rows and an investor-conference notice, dropped the guidance paragraph the table-intro rule over-matched, and orphaned a CEO quote at a split attribution; live was WORSE than stored on four EMR quarters. **Fix** (`data/filings.py`, 8 changes): (1) new `_is_table_intro_only` — the batch-156 `_TABLE_INTRO_RE` branch only fires when the intro sentence IS the whole line (no remainder after its first terminator, or the remainder has no `$`), restoring Emerson's Q3 FY2025 guidance paragraph ("Net and underlying sales guidance of ~3.5% ... GAAP EPS is now expected to be ~$4.08 ..."); measured: 220 intro lines in cache, 219 single-sentence, exactly 1 with `$` in the continuation; (2) new `_is_lede_single_value_row` — an unterminated, non-bullet, ≤120-char line with exactly 1 `$` figure ending in a bare `$` value token, label 2–12 words with no finite verb and not ending in a preposition, catching "Operating Cash Flow ~$3.6B", "Free Cash Flow ~$3.2B", "Discrete taxes related to AspenTech transaction $0.09", "IEEPA tariff refunds - ~($0.11)"; participles "provided"/"reported"/"related"/"adjusted" are deliberately excluded from the verb list so "Net cash provided by operating activities $ 3,803" still drops; (3) new `_is_lede_split_quote_attribution` as a third finish-item trigger (quote verb + line ends in a role title, no comma between verb and role) plus `_lede_quote_is_open` suppressing the sentence-terminated break while a quote is open, restoring EMR 2024-09-30's full CEO quote; (4) new `_INVESTOR_CONFERENCE_NOTICE_RE` ("will hold/host ... investor conference", `(?! call)` guard) plus `_is_lede_conf_call_heading` for "Conference Call and Upcoming Events" / "Webcast and Conference Calls"; (5) `_LEDE_TABLE_HEADER_METRIC_RE` gains EBITA/EBIT plus an optional trailing footnote digit, `_is_lede_table_header` word floor 4→3 with bare-year transparency, catching "Adjusted Segment EBITA4" and FICO's "Fourth Quarter Fiscal 2024 GAAP Revenue" ("GAAP Results" stays — "Results" is not a metric word, a judgment item); (6) the single-value predicate's last-word guard extends "to" → {"to", "of"} — the first blast showed it dropping wrapped summary lines ("Adjusted* Diluted EPS of $2.58", Citi's "BOOK VALUE PER SHARE OF $99.08", Duke's "reported and adjusted EPS of $1.44"); measured: 140 "of" candidates, every one sampled a prose summary line, while "at"/"for" have zero attested candidates and stay out; (7) `_BARE_CELL_RE` gains a lone `$` amount ("$ 0.44", "$ 1,251", "$(502)") — CSX's recon-table EPS cell survived as a lone bare value once the row above it dropped, a residue swap; a metric value carries a scale word or suffix ("$50.2 billion", "$1.2B"), which the number-only class never matches, so the batch-157 lone-value rule is untouched; (8) `_is_bare_numbered_footnote` cap 500→1000 — Emerson's 699-char Copeland footnote survived as the tail once the guidance rows dropped; ## Batch 184 (2026-11-03): CMG all-caps sub-headline dropped as a "Highlights" heading; 2 stored rows repaired

**Fix (`src/tickerlens/data/filings.py`):** `_HIGHLIGHTS_HEADING_RE`'s leading branch is narrowed from bare `^highlights?\b` to heading-shaped tails (`of|from|for|include` + unterminated rest) — the old form matched ANY line starting with the word, so Chipotle's all-caps sub-headlines ("HIGHLIGHTS RETURN TO POSITIVE COMPARABLE SALES AND TRANSACTIONS IN JUNE", Q2 2025; "HIGHLIGHTS POSITIVE COMPARABLE SALES", Q3 2025) were dropped everywhere the regex means "heading". Full-cache survey of every `^highlights` line (17 distinct): the narrowed rule keeps all real headings (bare "Highlights"/"HIGHLIGHTS", "HIGHLIGHTS OF SEGMENT RESULTS", "HIGHLIGHTS FROM THE QUARTER/YEAR", "Highlights from the report include:", "Highlights for the … are listed below:") and excludes the two CMG sub-headlines, Delta's terminated pointer sentence ("Highlights of the March quarter, … are on page five …" — terminator guard) and Airbnb's flattened-prose fragment ("highlights based suggested destinations …" — tail-word guard). The trailing-word branch is unchanged ("Selected Revenue Highlights", "Q1 2025 Financial Highlights (unaudited)"). Two coordinated changes were needed once the regex narrowed: (1) `_is_lede_heading_fragment` gains a carve-out — an all-caps line leading with "Highlights" that is NOT a highlights heading per the regex is a sub-headline, not a ≤4-word heading fragment (the Q3 fragment is 4 words; the real heading shapes "HIGHLIGHTS", "HIGHLIGHTS OF SEGMENT RESULTS", "HIGHLIGHTS FROM THE QUARTER" still drop); "RETURN" is deliberately NOT added to `_LEDE_HEADLINE_VERB_RE` — "Return on Equity" table labels would escape the table-header predicate. (2) The section collector splits the regex's two duties: `_HIGHLIGHTS_SECTION_ANCHOR_RE` keeps the broad pattern as the "a section starts here" signal (the CMG sub-headline anchored the section serving the dateline + bullets + quote + prose; Delta's pointer sentence anchored the quotes section — narrowing the anchor lost both, the first blast showed the CMG lede truncating at its 6-line budget and DAL flipping to the "Operations, Network and Fleet" section), and an anchor that is a leading-"Highlights" sub-headline (all-caps, unterminated, not a real heading) is prepended as the section's first content line — a terminated prose pointer stays a heading-only anchor. The 12-line section cap now counts FOLLOW lines only, so the prepended anchor cannot push the section's last line off (Chipotle's revenue prose); sections without a prepended anchor are byte-identical by construction.

**Tests:** 6 new in `tests/data/test_filings_batch184.py` (regex heading shapes + sub-headline/prose/fragment negatives, heading-fragment carve-out units, CMG Q2/Q3 synthetic end-to-end with the sub-headline served as the section's first line and all bullets surviving). Full suite: **1367 passed**. `git diff --check` clean.

**Blast radius (cache-only old-vs-new over 1,699 exhibits, `hidden_files/blast184c/base` → `new`):** **2 diffs, 1,697 byte-identical, 0 content losses** (verified programmatically — every old line survives in new). Both diffs are pure one-line additions of the CMG sub-headline; DAL is byte-identical. The first blast (regex narrowing alone, before the section-anchor fix) had 3 diffs and 2 real losses — the CMG sections collapsed to the 6-line lede and DAL flipped sections — which is what forced the anchor split; the second blast (anchor + prepend, old 12-line cap) had the same 2 diffs but dropped each quarter's trailing revenue-prose paragraph to the cap, which the follow-line-only cap fixed.

**Repair (`scripts/batch184_repair.py`, batch-183 gates, no gate change needed):** 2 targets, live==blast verified per target; **2 repaired, 0 skipped** — CMG 2025-06-30 (427→1748) and CMG 2025-09-30 (1553→1590), DB-verified. The 2025-06-30 row was also a truncation repair: its stored text held only the contact lines, headline, sub-headline, dateline, intro and one bullet from a pre-batch-179 extraction, while live serves the full section (7 bullets, CEO quote, revenue prose); the stored contact lines and the "Second quarter highlights, year over year:" intro are already defect lines under the gates (batch-179 contact, batch-177 colon heading).

**Live verification:** dev server restarted on :8123 to load the new code; **4/4 routes 200** (home, /company/CMG, /company/CMG/detail?quarter=Q2 FY2025, /company/CMG/detail?quarter=Q3 FY2025). Both detail pages serve the repaired sub-headlines (2× each) plus the revenue bullets. ## Batch 185 (2026-11-03): BRO orphaned flattened-table labels dropped; EOG supplement packet stays None; 5 stored rows repaired

**Fix (`src/tickerlens/data/filings.py`):** Table mode for the lede loop, in four parts. (1) `_is_lede_flat_table_label` / `_is_lede_flat_table_label_shape`: a flattened table row's label (5–6 words, figure-less, unterminated) directly below a table header block (period header, table heading/header/title, units line, recon heading, bare period fragment) is skipped and enters table mode — Brown & Brown flattens recon tables one cell per line, so the label's `$-value` rows drop as a fragment run and the label is the only table line left; the old `_is_lede_table_label` (batch 160) could not see it because its next line is the next label, not a data row. The label-run prev branch was tried in v1 and removed: it mistook wrapped sub-headlines for label runs (AAPL/AXP). (2) `_is_lede_table_mode_residue`: inside table mode, an unterminated or colon-ended line is another table row (however long), a "Note:" line is a footnote even when terminated, a company-name line ending in a corporate suffix is residue (its period is an abbreviation), and a terminated line ending in "respectively." that carries a 4-digit year is a flattened multi-column row ("Treasury stock, at cost 20 shares at 2024, 20 shares at 2023, respectively."). Terminated prose and bullets still end the mode and are collected (CSX). (3) `_is_lede_nongaap_section_heading`: in table mode only, the bare heading "Non-GAAP Financial Measures" breaks the loop — the definitions prose under it is boilerplate. It sits at the top of the loop body because `_is_lede_title_line` (batch 178) also matches the bare heading and would skip it before a later break could run. (4) `table_seen` gate: prose reached in table mode with no content collected and no real table row seen since the mode started is filler after a TOC, not narrative after a table — the loop breaks instead of collecting it (batch 126: a TOC-only packet must stay None). A real table leaves data rows / unit lines / period headers behind, so a quote or paragraph after it is still collected; bullets are exempt. Lone "—" (em dash) joins `_is_lone_bullet_marker` (batch 140 covered "–" only): an empty table cell that was collected, clearing table mode and letting the next labels in.

## Batch 187 (2026-11-03): AJG 2026 dash-led table value rows recognized as data rows; CEO quotes now served; 2 stored rows repaired

**Fix (`src/tickerlens/data/filings.py`):** `_is_lede_dash_value_row` — a dash-led wordless line whose every token is a dash placeholder or a figure token, with ≥1 figure ("– – 65 33 87 44 0.25 0.13", "— — 84 30 113 41 0.33 0.12", and the single-figure "– – – 1 – – – –") — is a data row, exempt from the batch-175 bullet guard in two places: `_is_lede_data_row` (returns True before the guard) and `_is_lede_table_line` (whose own top bullet guard returned False before the data-row check was ever reached — the first version of this fix, with only the `_is_lede_data_row` exemption, still served the rows; the end-to-end test caught it). A bullet always carries words, so the wordless shape can never be a bullet. Once the values row counts as a data row, its label line is caught by the batch-160 `_is_lede_table_label` next-line check. Terminated lines are prose, dashes-only lines are lone bullet markers, and figure-led lines are already data rows, so the predicate is exactly the dash-led wordless class.

## Batch 188 (2026-11-04): AJG 2026 table header lines dropped; glossary + safe-harbor tails skipped; 22 stored rows repaired

**Fix (`src/tickerlens/data/filings.py`):** closes batch 187's carried-forward item — the two AJG 2026 header lines — plus the two residue classes the freed budget exposed. (1) `_is_lede_table_header`: the capital-led check now strips leading `(` / trailing `)` before the `isupper` check, so the parenthesized capital "(Loss)" in "Net Earnings (Loss) EBITDAC Diluted Net Earnings" is capital-led (a parenthesized lowercase word still fails). (2) New `_is_lede_summary_results_title` (+ `_LEDE_SUMMARY_RESULTS_ANCHOR_RE`): a line starting "Summary of Financial Results" plus an optional " - <period>" tail of period/date tokens only — covers all 12 cached variants (First/Second/Third/Fourth Quarter, wrapped "Fourth", "Six-Months ended June 30", "Nine-Months ended September 30", "Year Ended December 31"), all AJG by full-cache survey — wired into `_is_lede_table_title` before the figure guard. The lede loop skips the summary title, never breaks on it: in older AJG quarters the dateline tail is collected as content ahead of the title, so break-on-content fired on boilerplate and lost the CEO quote in 10 quarters. (3) Open-quote finish rule: `finish_item` now also treats an open quote as unfinished (`_lede_quote_is_open`), since dropping the title shifts the 6-line budget boundary and cut the AJG 2024 quotes mid-sentence. (4) `_lede_quote_is_open` rewritten as a stack (curly open pushes, curly close pops, straight quote pops if depth>0 else pushes): the old by-kind count read mixed curly-open/straight-close quotes (BMY, CTAS) as open and pulled table chrome. (5) `_is_lede_recon_heading`: strips trailing parenthetical groups + colon before the guards and the word floor drops 2→1 — catches AJG's segment recon headings ("Brokerage Segment Reported GAAP to Adjusted Non-GAAP Reconciliations (dollars in millions):", "(continued)" variants). This revises a batch-182 test pin ("Reconciliations (continued) (dollars in millions):" is now a heading — it heads AJG's continued recon tables; the NOI caption pin still holds). (6) `_is_lede_report_pointer`: two new branches — `See` + "beginning on page" (AJG recon-table pointer; 7 distinct lines, all AJG) and `See Attachment` + "to this press release for" (ED; 10 distinct, all ED), plus the Citi "percentage comparisons throughout this press release are calculated for" methodology note (11 distinct, all C). (7) The recon-heading branch now enters table mode, so AJG's nested recon-label chains ("Base Commissions and Fees" / "Less supplemental revenues …") are table-mode residue; terminated prose after the table still clears the mode and is collected (CSX quote precedent). (8) `_ADMIN_LINE_RE`: new alternative for an all-caps title + page marker ("CON EDISON REPORTS 2026 FIRST QUARTER EARNINGS page 2"; 14 distinct, all ED) — the `(?-i:...)` scope matters because the regex is IGNORECASE. Blast follow-ups: (9) new `_glossary_def_block_len` — a run of ≥3 consecutive "Term - definition" lines (digit-free short term, terminated sentence) is the adjustment-item glossary after the recon tables (AJG's "Interest, banking costs and debt - …", EFX's "Antitrust litigation costs - …"); cache survey finds only 4 such runs, all glossaries, and datelines are excluded by the no-digits/no-month-name guards (a "Bethesda, MD - April 20, 2026 - …" dateline also needs the definition-part month guard). (10) `_BOILERPLATE_RE` gains the safe-harbor intro itself — "This press release contains forward-looking statements …" (281 distinct intros in the cache; the bare "forward-looking" alternative only matched at line start, so ED's lede ran on into the safe-harbor paragraph).

**Tests:** 15 in `tests/data/test_filings_batch188.py` (12 original + 3 blast follow-ups: glossary-line shape + block-length run rule, forward-looking boilerplate intro) plus the revised batch-182 pin and the batch-187 dateline fixture. Full suite: **1407 passed**. `git diff --check` clean.

## Batch 190 (2026-11-04): compact period fragments are not data rows; AJG heading misclassification closed; 0 stored rows to repair

**Fix (`src/tickerlens/data/filings.py`):** `_is_lede_data_row` now exempts compact period fragments (`_is_lede_compact_period_fragment`: "4th Q 25", "9 Mths 25" — AJG's flattened summary-table column headers). The fragment's two "figures" are a quarter ordinal and a 2-digit year — period labels, not values — but the density rule read them as figures (2 of 3 tokens) and the line qualified, so `_is_lede_table_label`'s next-line check misread a heading directly above one ("Summary of Financial Results - Fourth Quarter") as a table label. `_is_lede_table_line` gains a compact-fragment branch in the same batch so the fragment stays table residue: the data-row branch was the only one catching it (period_header and table-cell both read False on it; recon and single-value rows read False too), and without the branch the exemption would have let the fragment leak into the lede as content. A service restart mid-blast stashed the fix without popping it; the stash was popped and the blast re-run cleanly with the fix present.

## Batch 191 (2026-11-04): table-mode paragraph head served with its tail; AJG pretax paragraph whole; 6 stored rows repaired

**Fix (`src/tickerlens/data/filings.py`):** new `_lede_wrapped_paragraph_head` + a residue-run tracker in the lede loop. AJG's pretax paragraph sits inside the summary-table region: its wrapped head lines are unterminated, so the loop skipped them as table-mode residue and only the terminated tail was served — a dangling fragment ("income taxes is shown on pages 14 and 15.") with no subject (batch 186's wrap-tail wart). When a terminated line is collected in table mode, the loop now scans the residue run back from its end (continuations are lowercase-led, a year wrap — "2024, the pretax impact ..." — is the one digit-led continuation allowed) to the first capital-led line, and prepends that suffix as the paragraph's head. Budget-neutral: only the tail counts against the 6-line budget, so the CEO quote after the paragraph keeps exactly the budget it had. The naive alternative (lowercase-led terminated lines as residue) would eat the CEO quote's own wrapped lines — this predicate only ever ADDS lines. A label can never be the head: the head must end mid-sentence (wrap tail per `_LEDE_WRAP_TAIL_RE`) while a label ends in "*" or ")" or a noun, and the scan passes over the orphaned table labels that precede the paragraph (the batch-185/186 label chains are in the same run — the first implementation required the whole run to be one paragraph and joined nothing; the suffix scan is what makes it work).

**Tests:** 5 in `tests/data/test_filings_batch191.py` (head shapes incl. year wrap and single-line head; negatives incl. orphaned-label pass-over, label-as-head, capital-led/short/terminated wraps, quote tail; 3 AJG real-exhibit end-to-end pins: Q3 2023, Q2 2024, Q4 2023). Pin is behavioral: the test file errors at collection on stashed old code. Full suite: **1418 passed**. `git diff --check` clean.

**Blast radius (cache-only old-vs-new over 1,699 exhibits, `scripts/batch175_cache_blast.py`, `/tmp/blast191/base` → `new`, persisted to `hidden_files/blast191/new`):** **10 diffs, 1,689 byte-identical, 0 content losses** — all AJG (2023-09-30 through 2025-12-31), all pure additions: every added line is pretax-paragraph narrative, nothing removed anywhere. The CEO quote survives in all 11 quarters that serve it (base and new identical on the quote line); the 2026 quarters are unchanged (single-line paragraph, no wrap). A service restart interrupted the first blast attempt before it wrote anything; the blast was re-run in separate steps (new, stash+base, pop) and the fix verified present throughout.

**Repair (`scripts/batch191_repair.py`, batch-183 gates, no gate change):** 6 targets; **6 repaired, 0 skipped** — AJG 2024-09-30 (1812→2597), 2024-12-31 (1341→2123), 2025-03-31 (1323→2103), 2025-06-30 (1450→2233), 2025-09-30 (1585→2372), 2025-12-31 (1269→2049), live==blast verified, DB-verified. The 2023 quarters and 2024 Q1/Q2 have no stored rows (DB coverage starts 2024-09-30) and need no repair — live extraction serves them whole already.

## Batch 192 (2026-11-04): "#" and spaced-"*" footnote definitions are never highlights; APD's per-share "#" and non-GAAP "*" legends dropped; 1 stored row repaired

**Fix (`src/tickerlens/data/filings.py`):** two vocabulary changes in the shared footnote machinery, so every collection path (headline, lede, leading-bullet block, Highlights section) drops them. (1) `_FOOTNOTE_DEF_RE` gains a `^#\s*\S` shape: Air Products keys its per-share footnote "#" — "#Earnings per share is calculated ...", "#Per share amounts are calculated ...", "# Per share amounts are calculated ..." (attached and spaced forms across quarters) — and American Express its variance legend ("# - Denotes a variance of 100 percent or more."). Unlike "*", "#" never opens a bullet or prose in these exhibits (cache survey: only footnote definitions, legends, table fragments like DLR's "# of", and decorations "###" / "# # #"), so the spaced form is safe here where it is not for "*"; a bare "###" separator opens a block of length 1 (the next line is a heading, never a plausible wrap). (2) `_ASTERISK_NOTE_LEAD_RE` gains "Certain results": APD's non-GAAP legend — "* Certain results in this release include references to non-GAAP financial measures ..." (spaced, Q2 FY2026) and "*Certain results in this release, including in the highlights above, include references ..." (attached, Q1 FY2025) — was served as section content in Q2 FY2026 because the asterisk-note block is the one path that drops spaced-"*" notes and its lead list never covered this opener. The batch-165 "* Revenue grew 12 percent." bullet stays a bullet: the lead list stays note-shaped.

**Tests:** 7 in `tests/data/test_filings_batch192.py` (unit pins for the "#" shapes incl. the "###"-opens-a-block-of-one case, the "Certain results" opener in both forms, the batch-165 bullet negative; 3 real-exhibit end-to-end pins: APD Q2 FY2026, APD Q3 FY2025, AXP FY2023). Pin is behavioral: 6/7 fail on stashed old code (the negative passes on both, as it should). Full suite: **1425 passed**. `git diff --check` clean.

**Blast radius (cache-only old-vs-new over 1,699 exhibits, `scripts/batch175_cache_blast.py`, `/tmp/blast192/base` → `new`, persisted to `hidden_files/blast192/new`):** **4 diffs, 1,695 byte-identical, 0 content losses** — all footnote removals plus the freed-budget prose they let in: APD 2023-12-31 ("#Earnings ..." out, the "For the quarter, on a non-GAAP basis ..." paragraph in), APD 2025-06-30 ("#Per share ..." out, the "Third quarter sales of $3.0 billion ..." paragraph in), APD 2026-03-31 ("# Per share ..." and "* Certain results ..." out; tail changes "Fiscal…" to the full "Fiscal 2026 Second Quarter Results by Business Segment" heading as the 4000-char cap no longer truncates it), AXP 2023-12-31 ("# - Denotes ..." out, the "Consolidated expenses ..." paragraph in). A re-run of the blast after the guidance-filter revert (below) is byte-identical to the first new run.

**Repair (`scripts/batch192_repair.py`, batch-183 gates, no gate change):** 3 targets; **1 repaired, 2 skipped** — APD 2025-06-30 (1617→1912), live==blast verified, DB-verified. The "#" lines are defect lines via `_opens_footnote_def` (the new regex shape) and the "*" line via `_is_lede_table_line` (`_LEDE_FOOTNOTE_GLYPH_RE` matches spaced "*"), so preservation holds. ## Batch 193 (2026-11-04): guidance collector skips footnote blocks in-loop and ends the section at the next section heading; 113 stored guidance rows repaired

**Fix (`src/tickerlens/data/filings.py`, all in `extract_management_guidance`, now an index loop so blocks can be skipped whole):** (1) in-loop footnote skip — `_footnote_def_block_len` / `_rounding_note_block_len` / `_asterisk_note_block_len` (the Highlights section collector's machinery) drops APD's `#` per-share definition and `* Certain results` legend, AFL's `*See Non-U.S. GAAP...` pointer, AKAM's `** Cloud Infrastructure...` note, AMT's `(1)For additional discussion...` table footnote, and the `*Management is unable to reconcile` disclaimer. (2) next-section heading break (`_is_guidance_next_section_heading` + `_is_guidance_section_end_follower`): a short (≤90) unterminated title-case line, not itself a guidance heading, heading a bullet / dateline / long terminated $-less prose line — with exemptions for guidance vocabulary (ADP/ABBV sub-sections), period labels (ACN `Second Quarter Fiscal 2024`), datelines incl. split across lines (DG), a line after a lone bullet marker (CMG flattened `·` lists), non-title-case metric labels (DUOL), bare domains (CrownCastle.com footer), and paren-word table headers (`(Prior)`, `(Updated)`, incl. attached digit `(Updated)4` — BMY); a footnote block never swallows a guidance-vocabulary line (CARR's `1 Excludes NORESCO` block chained onto the `2026 Guidance` header). (3) signal gate (`_guidance_has_signal`): the break fires only once collected lines carry guidance (a bullet, a $/% figure, or expect/anticipat/forecast) — Crown Castle's OUTLOOK opens with a safe-harbor intro + Restructuring Plan Update before its outlook table. (4) anchor (`_is_guidance_heading`): tolerates an attached footnote digit on COMPOUND headings only (`Financial Guidance1` — DAL; bare word + digit `Guidance5` is CRM's table column header); rejects anchors ending in sentence punctuation + closing quote (DAL's quote fragment); plain terminated lines stay anchors (GEV's wrapped segment-outlook entry). (5) skips: `-more-` page markers and repeated release-title lines (AAL). (6) breaks added: `quarterly\s+conference\s+call` in `_FINANCIAL_TABLE_START_RE` (Axon); call/webcast notices via `_is_lede_call_announcement`, guarded `_CONFERENCE_CALL_NOTICE_RE` (match must start within first 120 chars — Biogen's FX line ends `...will be provided on the conference call and webcast.` and must not break), and `will host... webcast` (CHD). (7) all-caps period labels no longer break collection (`Q4 FY25`, `FY25` — CRM's table headers). Repair spot-check (before the DB repair) caught two more tail shapes the blast's junk check missed: Applied Materials' guidance section is followed by its results segment table (`First Quarter Reportable Segment Information` — now a `_FINANCIAL_TABLE_START_RE` alternative, up to five words may precede it) and General Mills' notice shape (`pre-recorded management remarks ... webcasted question and answer session` — new `_CALL_QA_SESSION_RE`, same 120-char guard; the `pre-recorded remarks` form without "management" covers the notice's continuation line).

## Batch 194 (2026-11-05): "-more-" page markers end the highlights lede at the next section heading; truncated-tail repair exemption; 2 stored rows repaired

**Fix (`src/tickerlens/data/filings.py`, `extract_press_release_highlights`):** the lede loop and the Highlights section collector never skipped the `-more-` page-continuation marker — the guidance collector gained the skip in batch 193, but the highlights paths did not, so APD Q2 FY2026 served `-more-` plus the next section's heading (`Fiscal 2026 Second Quarter Results by Business Segment`) as highlights. (1) `-more-` lines are skipped in both loops (never content, never budget — the lede loop previously swallowed them as table lines, the section collector as content). (2) A heading-shaped line right after a `-more-` ends the lede/section (`_is_more_page_section_heading`: ≤90 chars, unterminated, title-case, no $/%, not a bullet/dateline/all-caps line), when the previously collected line is terminated — the heading's content belongs to the next section. A paragraph wrapped across the page break is never heading-shaped (its head is sentence-case, or its previous line is unterminated), a quote-attribution tail ends with a comma or colon, and table mode suppresses the break (batch 185: prose after a table is still collected). (3) A bare company-name page header after the marker is skipped transparently in the same window (`_is_trailing_company_signature`: ≤6 capitalized words ending in a corporate suffix — CarMax's `CarMax, Inc.` + `Page 2 of 13` head page 2; the skip freed a section slot the signature would otherwise fill, and the CEO quote after it stays out via the section cap). (4) `_clean_highlights_result` also pops a trailing `-more-` or a trailing company-name signature (paths that bypass the loops, e.g. shareholder-letter / flat-table modes).

## Batch 195 (2026-11-05): bullet content gated by headings is served — leading-bullet intro skip + guidance-heading bullet look-ahead; 4 stored rows repaired

**Fix (`src/tickerlens/data/filings.py`, `extract_press_release_highlights`):** APD Q1 FY2025 served only its headline + 1 dividend bullet (310 chars) although the release opens with its two top financial bullets and a Guidance bullet pair — two heading shapes gated the bullets. (1) The leading-bullet block broke on its first line, the colon-ended list intro `Q1 FY25 (comparisons versus prior year):` — the lede loop already skips such headings (batch 177) but the leading-bullet block never did, so the two financial bullets under it never competed. The block now skips a `_is_lede_colon_heading` line before any bullet is collected (same position as the dateline/period-header skips); when the line after the intro is not a bullet the else branch breaks the block exactly as before, so the skip alone can never create a block. (2) The Highlights section collector broke on the bare `Guidance` sub-heading via `_is_lede_table_heading` — the same predicate that ends a section at AppLovin's `First Quarter 2026 Financial Guidance Summary` table title. A guidance heading that introduces bullets is a sub-heading, not a table title, so the break now fires only when the next content line is not a bullet (`_next_content_line_is_bullet`: first non-admin line after the heading, `•`/`◦`/`·` count — a page-number admin line between heading and bullets does not hide them). The heading itself is collected as a label line (the stored APD row carries it as a non-defect line, and ADBE's served output already keeps section headings mid-section). The same look-ahead applies in the leading-bullet block's continuation branch. AppLovin's shape — guidance-summary heading followed by a table row — still breaks the section.

**Tests:** 8 in `tests/data/test_filings_batch195.py` (look-ahead unit pins incl. admin-line skip and `·` bullets; leading-block intro skip + intro-without-bullets negative; synthetic APD shape for both fixes; AppLovin-shape negative; leading-block guidance sub-heading; real-exhibit pin: APD 2024-12-31 live >2000 chars with the guidance bullets and no `-more-`/footnote lines). Pin is behavioral: on stashed old code the synthetic leading-intro case serves no GAAP bullet (498 chars), the synthetic guidance case serves no guidance bullet (256 chars), and APD live is 310 chars. Full suite: **1478 passed**. `git diff --check` clean.

**Blast radius (cache-only old-vs-new over 1,699 exhibits, `scripts/batch175_cache_blast.py`, `/tmp/blast195/base` → `new`, persisted to `hidden_files/blast195/new`):** **29 diffs, 1,670 byte-identical, 0 None flips (36 None on both sides), 0 content losses in the strict sense** — APD ×8 (5 wins incl. the target, 310→2711; 2 ranking flips: Q3/Q4 FY2024 business bullets displaced by the financial bullets, per the batch-102 financial-density rule; 2 washes: Q1 FY2024 financial bullets survive only as prose while the section adds the sales paragraph + CEO quote, Q3 FY2025 trailing sales paragraph dropped by the leading block's item-close rule), CPB ×2 pure additions (shareholder-return + Investor Day bullets, dateline paragraph), CRM ×1 pure addition (4 guidance bullets + `Guidance` label), CSCO ×4 completions and better bullets (`News Summary:` colon intro; wrapped bullets now whole, incl. `Cisco's technologies` wrap tail), DIS ×10 full bullet runs + CEO quotes (the lede 6-line budget starved them; the leading block now wins — one wart: the `(1 ) Diluted EPS excluding certain items...` footnote line in DIS 2023-09-30 only, where the `(1 )` shape with a space defeats the footnote regexes), NFLX ×3 pure additions (closing bullets + `Our summary results, and forecast for QX, are below.`), SKYD ×1 blank-lines-only (winner path change, identical content).

**Repair (`scripts/batch195_repair.py`, batch-194 gates + 2 refinements):** 16 targets (the blast diffs with stored rows); **4 repaired, 12 skipped gate-correct** — APD 2024-12-31 (3486→2711), APD 2025-03-31 (808→2399), CPB 2025-08-03 (644→1371), CRM 2025-07-31 (1177→1167, content-identical except bullet spacing). Gate refinements, both documented in the script: (a) **colon-heading exemption** — a stored line matching `_is_lede_colon_heading` is heading material, not content, so it counts as droppable (without it the APD Q2 FY2025 repair is refused on its `Q2 FY25 Summary of Results (comparisons versus prior year):` intro although every content line survives); (b) **bullet-space normalization** — `norm()` strips the space after a leading bullet glyph, so a stored `• Data Cloud ...` line matches the live `•Data Cloud ...` line (without it the CRM repair is falsely refused — its stored row is otherwise byte-identical in content to the new live). Gates otherwise unchanged: live fetched from EDGAR via the service (batch-192 pattern); source matches stored; live ≥200 chars and differs from stored; live==blast where a blast file exists; live carries no defect lines; every stored non-defect ATOMIC line survives in live. ## Batch 196 (2026-11-05): bare-word guidance headings with a footnote digit are headings (CI's OUTLOOK2 rescued); structural tail-drop repair for 194 stale stored guidance rows

**Fix (`src/tickerlens/data/filings.py`, `_is_guidance_heading`):** Cigna's outlook section is headed by the bare word with a superscript footnote digit (`OUTLOOK2` in text — the HTML is `OUTLOOK<sup>2</sup>`), so CI's 2024-12-31, 2025-03-31 and 2025-09-30 quarters extracted None while every other CI quarter (compound `2025 OUTLOOK2`, already accepted) served guidance. Batch 193's digit-strip path required a space in the stripped line (DAL's compound `Financial Guidance1` vs CRM's `Guidance5` table column header); the space requirement is dropped — the stripped line only has to match the heading regex. A census of the 1,699-exhibit cache finds exactly six bare-word+digit guidance headings: CI's three OUTLOOK2 (real section headings, followed by outlook prose + metrics table), CRM_2024-10-31's two Guidance5 (column headers inside the guidance section — the extractor already anchors on the earlier `Guidance` heading, so they can never become the anchor), and EMR_2025-09-30's `Guidance 2026` (also after the earlier `2026 Outlook` anchor). A bare-word+digit line can only anchor when no earlier guidance heading exists — i.e. when it IS the section heading, as in CI. The batch-193 test asserting the old rule is updated to the new behavior with a pointer to the batch-196 tests.

**Tests:** 9 in `tests/data/test_filings_batch196.py` (OUTLOOK2/Guidance5/compound-digit unit pins; CI ×3 live pins; CRM/EMR no-reanchor pins; APD two-bullets pin). Full suite: **1487 passed** (1478 + 9 new). `git diff --check` clean.

**Blast (guidance-only old-vs-new over 1,699 exhibits, `scripts/batch196_gblast.py`, `hidden_files/blast196/base` → `new`, classified with `scripts/batch193_gblast_analyze.py`):** **799 vs 796 guidance texts, 0 lost, 3 gained (exactly CI's three quarters), 0 diff files, 0 removed lines, 0 added junk.** The extractor change touches nothing else in the cache.

**Repair (`scripts/batch196_repair.py`, structural tail-drop gate):** batch 193 repaired 113 stored guidance rows but skipped 294 gate-correct — its preservation gate ("live must keep every stored line") cannot tell results narrative from guidance content, so rows whose stored text is pre-193 over-collection (guidance bullets followed by up to ~3,800 chars of results narrative, segment prose, call notices and footnote legends, cut at the 4,000-char cap) waited for a follow-up. This is that follow-up — as a repair gate, not the multi-section merge the batch-193 docstring guessed at (see below). **194 repaired, 103 skipped gate-correct** (of 1,253 stored guidance rows; 699 have no cached exhibit, 257 live==stored or None after repair). Biggest shrinks: APD ×8 (3999→~230 each — the stored text was 2 guidance bullets + the whole results narrative), GILD ×8, GPC ×6, FDX, DPZ, ET 2024-12-31 (3449→304), CDNS, AXON, CL. Live-longer 8 (genuine gains, e.g. BMY 2026-06-30 1504→3071: the non-GAAP line-item table re-joined + 2 guidance paragraphs freed). Gate: live != stored, ≥100 chars, == blast196/new, no defect lines in live; every dropped stored paragraph (non-defect, bullet- and digit-spacing-insensitive 60-char key absent from live) must sit at/after the first next-section heading in the stored paragraph sequence, or be itself a next-section heading, or match a known junk shape (footnote/call/boilerplate/`-more-`/page/decoration/dateline/bare date/webcast-host/replay logistics/safe-harbor disclaimer/About prose); a dropped paragraph matching a guidance heading blocks the repair; added live paragraphs must carry guidance signal ($, %, guidance word, bullet) or be a short table fragment, and must not be call-logistics lines. Dry-run re-run after `--write`: 0 would repair, 451 live==stored, 103 skipped (unchanged). DB-verified: APD 2025-09-30 is 219 chars (heading + 2 bullets), GILD 2024-12-31 644, ET 2024-12-31 304; FSLR 2024-12-31 unchanged at 1741, CI 2025-09-30 unchanged at 1879.

**Merge evaluated, not pursued:** the carried-forward guidance multi-section merge was investigated first (probes 1–3): 244 exhibits have 2+ guidance headings, but the second sections are mostly duplicates or repeats — APD's later `Outlook` prose duplicates the first section's EPS/capex numbers, CRM's second section is a margin-reconciliation bridge already summarized in the first section's bullets, DAL has only one heading and its stored text already equals live, AMZN's second heading (`Second Quarter 2024 Guidance`) is a sub-heading inside the already-collected first section, ADP's later headings repeat the same section, AMT's first section already hits the 4,000-char cap, and CCI has 7–19 `Outlook` headings (segment tables) where a naive merge would be a disaster; APD's `Outlook for Investing Activities` is a cash-flow-statement false anchor. The higher-value work was the stale-rows repair above. The merge stays carried forward for ACN-like cases only (summary bullets + later detail tables).
