## Batch 197 (2026-11-05): bullet-run splitting for ●/○ separators + bullet exemption for the page-line guard — Instacart (×10) and Booking (×7) highlights rescued from None; 13 stored rows repaired

**Fix (`src/tickerlens/data/filings.py`, `_split_bullet_runs`):** the batch-125 splitter only split ` • ` runs. Instacart's shareholder letter, Booking's release, and Affirm's letter join their bullet runs with ` ● ` (Instacart also ` ○ ` for sub-bullets), so the split never fired: all 10 cached CART quarters, all 7 BKNG quarters, and AFRM 2024-12-31 extracted None — their "Financial Highlights" bullets sit mid-line in a mega-line no anchor ever sees. The separator regex is now `\s+[•●○]\s+` (≥2 separators, line >300 chars, as before); continuation pieces keep the `• ` prefix so downstream bullet logic is unchanged. The downstream glyph set already treats ●/○ as bullets (`_BULLET_LEAD_RE`, the lone-marker test), so this only extends the batch-125 mechanism to the glyphs the documents actually use.

**Fix 2 (same file, page-line guard):** six CART quarters still extracted None after the split — their sections extract fine, but the batch-105 page-line guard (no headline + a line over 1000 chars) discards the whole section because the section's narrative paragraphs are flattened mega-lines too (1200–2500 chars a line). A bullet-led line now exempts the paragraph from the guard. The narrowing's blast delta is exactly those 6 CART quarters — no other exhibit moves (Ford's early risk-factor releases, COIN's decks, EOG's image-only packets all stay None through their own gates).

**Tests:** 11 in `tests/data/test_filings_batch197.py` (●/○ split unit pins incl. short/single-separator negatives; CART ×3, BKNG, AFRM, COIN live pins; EOG + Ford-early still-None pins). Full suite: **1498 passed** (1487 + 11). `git diff --check` clean.

## Batch 198 (2026-11-05): line-leading bullet glyphs get a space after them in `_html_to_text` — glued `•Net sales` / `◦HOKA` / `▪Segment OI` bullets now render spaced on every served card; 215 stored guidance rows repaired

**Fix (`src/tickerlens/data/filings.py`, `_html_to_text`):** inline formatting tags are stripped with no separator (deliberate — DFIN span-splitting, "RIS</span><span>K FACTORS"), so a bullet glyph in its own `<font>`/`<span>` glued to its item text (`&#8226;</font><font>Net sales...` → `•Net sales...`). Stored rows from the older extractor already carry the spaced form (`• Net sales...`), and a census of the blast outputs showed the glued form on ~1,400 of the 1,699 cached exhibits (highlights: `•` 6,225 lines, `◦` 528, `■` 71, `●` 65, `–` 55, `▪` 49; guidance: 402 files, 2,219 lines). A line-start normalization now inserts the space after a leading bullet glyph (`•●○◦▪■·`); dashes (`–—`) are gated on a following letter so a table negative (`–$5`, `–123`) is untouched. The strip runs before the final `.strip()` — a leading space from a stripped opening tag otherwise hides the first line's bullet from the `^` anchor (caught by a unit pin). Mid-line bullets are untouched.

**Correction to a batch-197 carry-forward:** "DIS/DECK `◦`-led guidance bullets dropped by the collector" was wrong — the collector collects them (DECK `◦ HOKA` / `◦ UGG` and DIS `◦ Entertainment` / `◦ Adjusted EPS` all serve live). What was actually stale was the stored rows, refused by the batch-196 repair gate for a different reason (see below).

**Tests:** 9 in `tests/data/test_filings_batch198.py` (per-glyph unit pins incl. dash-before-digit negative; first-line pin; DECK/DIS guidance live pins; AAL highlights live pin). Full suite: **1507 passed** (1498 + 9 new). `git diff --check` clean. 14 pre-existing tests in batches 155/156/158/165/169/170/172 pinned the glued form in expected outputs; their expectations are updated to the spaced form with a batch-198 pointer comment each. Fixture inputs stay glued (they exercise the normalization path) and the direct regex/helper unit tests on glued literals (`_LEDE_WRAP_TAIL_RE`, `_is_inline_bullet_start`) are untouched — they pin behavior on stored text, which still carries the glued form.

**Blast (cache-only old-vs-new over 1,699 exhibits, `scripts/batch198_classify.py`, `hidden_files/blast198/hl_old` → `hl_new`, `g_old` → `g_new`):** highlights **1,117 diffs, all spacing-only, 0 None-flips, 0 content changes**; guidance **318 diffs, all spacing-only, 0 None-flips, 0 content changes**. The classifier de-spaces both sides before comparing (the first version de-spaced only the new text and false-positived on lines already spaced in old, e.g. CVS's `— David Joyner` attribution); the remaining "content" diffs are 4000-char cap shifts — the added spaces move the cut point one word earlier (CEG ×3, EQIX ×6, CARR/CCL/CLX/CPB/DASH/ECL/EL/ETN/FDX/GE in guidance), same content, tail differs by the last word(s) before the `…`.

**Repair (`scripts/batch198_repair.py`, the batch-196 structural tail-drop gate, unchanged except its norm):** **215 repaired, 85 skipped** (of 1,254 stored guidance rows; 699 have no cached exhibit; live==stored grew 255→470 after the write). Re-run after `--write`: 0 would repair. DB-verified: DIS 2025-12-27 3416→1708 (footnote-definition tail only — every guidance bullet survives), DECK ×4 (spacing-only, 1348→1355 etc.), MU ×4 (1188→539: the guidance table survives whole; the dropped tail is the `Investor Webcast` call notice + website boilerplate past the boundary), CRM 2025-01-31 138→217 (live adds a genuine guidance intro + `Q1 FY26` period label). Most repairs are pure spacing refreshes (stored glued from the batch-195 era vs live now spaced, +2 to +20 chars: AMZN ×9, DHI ×8, AMGN ×8, AVGO ×8, ADP ×8, CDNS ×10, AXON ×8, FTNT ×8, CMG ×9, ETN ×8, CL ×8, DKS ×8, CCL ×7, CLX ×7, EL ×5).

**Two norm bugs found in the batch-196 gate, both fixed in the batch-198 copy (the frozen batch-196 script is untouched):** (1) `norm196`'s `^•\s+` strip was start-of-string only — it fired on a stored paragraph compared standalone but never on a bullet mid-text in the flattened live string, so the gate refused every row whose stored/live bullet spacing differed in the "wrong" direction; the strip is now multiline and covers every bullet glyph, and runs BEFORE the whitespace collapse (collapsing first leaves no line starts for `^` — that ordering bug cost one dry-run cycle). (2) The digit-spacing canonicalization missed the parenthesized form — stored `income (1)` vs live `income(1)` (superscript footnote digit) — and the opening-quote form — stored `“ Our results...` vs live `“Our results...` (the same inline-tag gluing, typographically fine, so canonicalized in the gate only, not the extractor). Those two fixes unblocked DIS ×5 (the Iger quote paragraphs now match instead of reading as dropped content).

## Batch 199 (2026-11-05): First Solar guidance gaps — rule-line follower, intro-sentence anchors, wrapped figure lines, "N." footnote keys, dividend-declaration section ends; 63 stored guidance rows repaired + 20 NULL rows filled

**Fixes (`src/tickerlens/data/filings.py`):** five defects, one ticker's guidance section — plus the blast-wide fallout each fix carries.
1. **Rule-line follower:** `_is_guidance_section_end_follower` now skips rule-only lines (`^[-–—_=*]{3,}$`) when advancing to the real follower, like admin lines. First Solar's guidance table ends with a rule directly after its last row, and the rule matched the dash-led bullet branch, so `Volume Sold 15.6GW to 16.3GW` read as a next-section heading and the break dropped it (and the safe-harbor paragraph after the footnote block). Corrects the batch-198 carry-forward attribution: the drop was the follower test, not the collector's signal check — the signal check never fires on the row.
2. **Intro-sentence anchors:** a guidance-intro sentence now anchors the section (`_GUIDANCE_INTRO_RE`: `^our (updated|revised)? \d{4} guidance\b.*(as follows|summarized below)`). First Solar's interim releases introduce the table with prose ("Our 2024 guidance has been updated as follows:", "Our 2026 guidance remains unchanged and is summarized below:") and no heading, so 9 of 11 cached FSLR quarters extracted None despite carrying a full table. A census of the 1,699-exhibit cache finds this shape only in FSLR, and no serving exhibit has one before its real anchor.
3. **Wrapped figure lines:** the collector's all-caps break now requires a two-letter word, so a wrapped table value on its own line (`$520M` — FSLR's split "Operating Expenses" row) no longer breaks collection mid-table. A real heading ("CONFERENCE CALL DETAILS") always carries one.
4. **"N." footnote keys:** `_is_bare_numbered_footnote` now allows an optional period after the key (`1.Assumes ...`, `3.Adjusted EBITDA reflects ...` — First Solar's 2026 footnote defs; `1. Adjustment item resides in ...` — the batch-198 carry-forward shape, spaced form). The capital-after-number guard is unchanged, so decimal-led figures (`1.5 million shares ...`) still never open a block.
5. **Dividend declarations end the section:** `_is_guidance_next_section_heading` now returns True for a dividend-declaration heading (`Dividend Declaration`, `Quarterly Dividend`, `Dividend Payment`). Its follower paragraph carries "$0.48 per share", so the $-less follower test never fired on it and the declaration was collected as guidance (COR, EXPE, ADI ×12, EBAY ×3).

**Tests:** 17 in `tests/data/test_filings_batch199.py` (rule-line follower unit pins, intro-heading positives/negatives, wrapped-figure live pin, all-caps-heading still-breaks synthetic pin, "N." key positives/negatives, 2026 footnote-def live pin, dividend-declaration live pins). Full suite: **1524 passed** (1507 + 17). `git diff --check` clean.

**Blast (cache-only old-vs-new over 1,699 exhibits, `scripts/batch199_cache_blast.py`, `hidden_files/blast199/g_old` → `g_new2`):** **76 diffs, 18 None-flips, 0 regressions in kind.** FSLR ×11 (the target: 9 None→table, 2 Volume-Sold rescues, 2025-12-31 unchanged). ## Batch 200 (2026-11-05): guidance section-end misses — colon-ended headings, quote-led followers, results-table period-header followers; 31 stored guidance rows repaired

**Fix (`src/tickerlens/data/filings.py`, `_is_guidance_next_section_heading` + `_is_guidance_section_end_follower`):** three defects, one defect class (the guidance section runs past its end into the next section).
1. **Colon-ended headings:** a trailing colon counted as sentence termination, so a colon-ended heading label never read as a next-section heading. `_is_guidance_next_section_heading` now treats a colon-ended line as unterminated for heading purposes (still gated on title case + the follower test, so a prose fragment like "We expect the following:" never qualifies). Cuts Cisco's "Editor's Notes:" + call-logistics bullets (×9 quarters), Disney's "Message From Our CEO:" + CEO quote (×7), GE's "Caution Concerning Forward Looking Statements:" + safe-harbor risk prose (×9). Guidance-word headings ("Q1 FY 2026 Guidance (1):", "Segment guidance is:") stay exempt via the vocabulary check, and period labels ("Fiscal Year 2027:") via the period-label check.
2. **Quote-led followers:** the follower test's `nxt[0].isupper()` failed on a quote-led paragraph (the opening curly quote is not a cased character), so a CEO quote after a heading never counted as a next-section follower. It now reads the first cased char. Bonus cut: Carnival's "Financing and Capital Activity" and "PROPEL: Powering Growth & Returns, Responsibly" headings (×2) — the PROPEL long-term-targets announcement no longer rides inside the FY2026 outlook.
3. **Colon + quotation follower:** Disney's Q2/Q3 FY2025 CEO quotes open with a wrapped fragment (no terminal punctuation on the first flattened line), so even the fixed follower test never fired. A colon-ended label followed by a quotation now ends the section outright — a speaker label, never guidance content. A non-colon heading + wrapped quote still does not end the section (pinned negative).
4. **Results-table period headers:** a period header ("Three months ended September 30", doubled "Three months ended ... Nine months ended ...") after a heading now counts as a section-end follower. Cuts GE Vernova's "Total Company Results" condensed-results-table tail (×11 quarters).

**Tests:** 19 in `tests/data/test_filings_batch200.py` (colon-heading unit pins incl. guidance-word / period-label / prose-fragment negatives, period-header follower pins, quote-led + colon-quote pins incl. the non-colon negative, real-exhibit pins for CSCO/DIS/GE/GEV). Pin is behavioral: 12 of 19 fail on stashed old code. Full suite: **1543 passed** (1524 + 19). `git diff --check` clean.

**Blast (cache-only old-vs-new over 1,699 exhibits, `scripts/batch200_cache_blast.py`, `hidden_files/blast200/g_old` → `g_new`):** **38 diffs, 0 None-flips, 0 added paragraphs — all pure tail cuts.** CSCO ×9 (Editor's Notes), DIS ×7 (CEO message), GEV ×11 (results table), GE ×9 (safe harbor), CCL ×2 (Financing / PROPEL). 10 of the 38 diffs have no DB row (cached exhibits for quarters the DB never seeded — CSCO 2023-10-28→2024-07-27, DIS 2024-06-29, GE 2024-03-31/2024-06-30, GEV 2024-03-31/2024-06-30, CCL 2024-02-29); the fix serves them correctly on next refresh. No guidance content lost in any diff.

**Repair (`scripts/batch200_repair.py`, the batch-196 gate + batch-200 widenings: the five boundary headings incl. both apostrophe forms of "Editor's Notes", plus a fill-path guard):** **31 repaired, 0 filled, 44 skipped** (re-run after `--write`: 0 would repair/fill). Repairs: DIS ×6 (CEO-message tails cut), CSCO ×5, GE ×7 (safe-harbor tails cut, 3997→1661–2785), GEV ×7 (results-table tails cut), CCL ×2 (2024-11-30 3998→2366 — stored ran through Sustainability + Other Recent Highlights; 2026-02-28 3995→1725 — PROPEL cut), DAL ×3 (124/208/121-char stubs synced to full live 3536/3999/3808 — the live text carries the known DAL narrative-tail over-collection, now stored, same precedent as batch-199 DLR), COIN 2025-12-31 (1174→1526 — stale table-only row synced to live, which adds the outlook prose and ends at the "Product Update" budget artifact). The carve-out: a heading whose final conjunct is "and Outlook" and whose topic word is a results noun (`_GUIDANCE_NARRATIVE_OUTLOOK_RE`: `<Topic> (Environment|Performance|Results) and Outlook`, optional trailing colon) is a narrative heading — it opens the commentary that FOLLOWS the guidance table, not the guidance section itself — so it may end the section, subject to the usual title-case + follower + has-signal gates. Pure outlook headings never match ("Guidance and Outlook" has no results noun; "2025 Outlook" / "Financial Outlook" have no "and Outlook" tail), and neither does DKS's "Foot Locker Business: Q4 2025 Commentary & Outlook" (colon prefix, period label, "Commentary", "&"), whose section is genuine outlook content.

## Batch 203 (2026-11-06): section-tagline follower ends the guidance section; COIN "Product Update" cut, 1 stored row repaired

**Fix (`src/tickerlens/data/filings.py`, `_is_guidance_section_end_follower`):** the batch-200 carry-forward (COIN "Product Update") is closed. "Product Update" is a real next-section heading, but its follower is a short tagline ("Building the future of finance, step by step" — 46 chars, unterminated) and the prose after it is wrapped, so its first flattened line is a fragment: neither the long-prose follower test (needs >= 60 chars, terminated, $-less) nor any other follower shape fired, and the heading was collected as guidance — the 30-line budget's last slot. A heading followed by a short sentence-case tagline and then a wrapped prose run that terminates within 6 lines and totals >= 100 chars now ends the section. A tagline is short (< 60), unterminated, carries no $/% (a figure line is a table row), is not a bullet/dateline/period header/quote, and is sentence-case, not title-case (a second heading/label is title-case: "Investor Relations", "CEO Comment", "BB+"). Two run guards keep the join from crossing a guidance table to a distant disclaimer paragraph: every non-terminating run line must be >= 50 chars (a wrapped prose line is near column width; a table row is short) and figure-sparse (at most one $/% character per 25 chars — GPC's "Global Automotive sales were $3.5 billion, up 0.8% ... with a 2.9% benefit ..." is 3 tokens in 110 chars; EL's "Forecasted/Adjusted EPS - Non-GAAP $3.10 - $3.35 $ 2.51 24% - 34%" is 4 in 68). The terminating line is exempt from both — it is the sentence tail and may be short or carry the sentence's figures. The unguarded version also cut BMY 2024-06-30 (2277 -> 388, losing the Diluted EPS $0.60-$0.90 row), EL 2026-06-30 (2256 -> 531, losing the assumptions bullets and the 12.7%-13.5% margin target), BMY 2024-03-31 and V 2023-09-30 (trailing table-row labels); the guards kill all four, and all four are pinned unchanged in the tests.

## Batch 204 (2026-11-06): guidance line budget becomes a backstop with a 20-line lookahead; mid-line sentence boundary in the tagline follower; 36 stored guidance rows repaired

**Fix (`src/tickerlens/data/filings.py`, `_MAX_GUIDANCE_LINES` + `_GUIDANCE_BUDGET_LOOKAHEAD_LINES`, `extract_management_guidance`):** the batch-203 carry-forward (COIN budget starvation) is closed with a budget rethink, not a number bump. A flat raise to 40 would also extend runaway sections (CSCO, ELV, ECL) by 10 lines of leakage, so the 30-line budget stays and gains a lookahead: when the budget is hit, the collector keeps going for up to 20 more kept lines, but only keeps them if a section-end break fires inside the window; if no break fires — or the document runs out (while-else path) — the extra lines are discarded and the 30-line cut stands. COIN's "Webcast Information" sits 6–8 lines past the cut and ADP's "Investor Webcast Today" ~20 lines past it, so both complete; a runaway section with no end in sight still stops at 30 lines. **Second fix (`_is_guidance_section_end_follower`, tagline run):** COIN 2025-09-30's "Product Update" prose wraps so the first sentence ends mid-line ("... rich app ecosystem. Coinbase is building ...") and the line-end termination test never fires — the 6-line run expires unterminated and the heading is collected as guidance. A mid-line boundary (lowercase letter + terminal punctuation + space + uppercase) now terminates the run; abbreviations ("U.S. GAAP", "No. 1") never match, and the check runs after the run guards, so a figure-dense line is still killed as a table row instead of terminating.

## Batch 205 (2026-11-06): results-boundary detector in the guidance main loop; DELL/ECL/DASH bleed cut, 17 stored guidance rows repaired

**Fix (`src/tickerlens/data/filings.py`, `_GUIDANCE_RESULTS_PERIOD_RE` + `_is_results_table_ahead` + direct break in `extract_management_guidance`):** the batch-204 carry-forward (DELL/ECL/DASH guidance sections bleed into the reported results table) is closed. Two parts. (1) Generalize `_GUIDANCE_RESULTS_PERIOD_RE`: the old shape only matched the dated lowercase form ("Three months ended January 31"), so it missed the dateless/combined form ("Three Months Ended Fiscal Year Ended" — DELL; "Three Months Ended Y/Y % Change Q/Q % Change" — DASH), the quarter form ("First Quarter Ended March 31" — ECL) and the standalone fiscal-year form. A forward guidance period says "Ending", never "Ended", so the past tense is the discriminator — and as a follower it only fires after a heading candidate, which is how DELL/DASH now break at their results headings ("Fourth Quarter ... Financial Results", "Q3 2026 Financial Performance"). (2) A direct break in the main loop on the period header itself: ECL's two outlook bullets run straight into its results table with no heading line at all. The break needs guidance signal already delivered (a guidance table's own period header sits before any signal — AKAM's "Three Months Ended" intro) and a results-table confirmation ahead — a unit line ("(in millions, ...; unaudited)"), a standalone "Change"/"Reported"/"Adjusted" column label, or Y/Y-Q/Q change columns; a "Low End High End" range label anywhere in the 8-line window refuses (two passes, so a guidance table that prints a unit line first is still refused). Without the confirmation a guidance table that happens to head its columns with "Ended" would be cut.

**Tests:** 17 in `tests/data/test_filings_batch205.py` (real-exhibit pins for DELL 2025-01-31, ECL 2025-03-31, DASH 2026-09-30, AKAM 2024-03-31 unchanged, GE 2025-06-30 completion; regex match/reject pins incl. the "Ending" negatives; `_is_results_table_ahead` unit pins incl. the range-label-after-unit-line refusal; synthetic docs for the direct break, the heading-path break, and both guards). ## Batch 206 (2026-11-06): page-footer furniture skip in the guidance collector; CCI tagline/domain lines cut, 8 stored guidance rows repaired

**Fix (`src/tickerlens/data/filings.py`, `_BARE_DOMAIN_RE` + in-loop skips in `extract_management_guidance`):** the batch-205 carry-forward (CCI stored guidance rows carry "The pathway to possible." / "CrownCastle.com" furniture lines, 2 lines per occurrence, 11 occurrences across 8 stored rows) is closed. CCI prints a page footer on every page of its releases — tagline, bare domain, "News Release continued: Page N" — and the OUTLOOK section spans pages, so footers fall mid-table inside the collected guidance. The "News Release continued" line was already skipped by `_ADMIN_LINE_RE` (its "news release" prefix); the tagline and the domain were not. A bare-domain line (a line that IS a domain, nothing else — no-spaces shape, so prose that merely mentions a domain never matches) is now skipped like an admin line, and a short (<= 60 chars) terminated sentence-case line immediately before a bare domain is skipped as the footer tagline. The lookahead keeps the tagline skip narrow: a genuine short guidance sentence is skipped only when the very next line is a bare domain — the footer signature. Figure lines ($/%/digits) and title-case lines never match, so a table row or a heading before a domain pointer survives. Both skips are in-loop, so the 30-line budget is spent only on kept lines (the batch-193 lesson) — which also completes four previously budget-truncated CCI tables. **Gate support (`scripts/batch200_repair.py`, `norm196`):** the letter-footnote-marker spacing collapse — CCI's stored rows render "Site rental billings (a)" where live has "Site rental billings(a)", an older-extractor spacing artifact of the same class as the batch-198 digit rule, so the key must match both forms (symmetric canonicalization, comparison-only, not an extractor change). **Repair support (`scripts/batch206_repair.py`, `_added_block_ok`):** a bare figure-range table row ("1,640 to 1,670" — CCI 2025-12-31's FFO row) carries no $/% because the table's unit line carries the units; it now counts as guidance signal.

**Tests:** 16 in `tests/data/test_filings_batch206.py` (real-exhibit pins for the 7 cached CCI quarters incl. exact para counts, regex match/reject pins, synthetic docs for the footer-pair drop, the domain-alone drop, and the three guards: short sentence without a domain kept, figure sentence before a domain kept, title-case line before a domain kept, prose mentioning a domain kept). Pin is behavioral: 14 of 16 fail on stashed old code (the 2 that pass are the negative pins — short sentence without a domain, prose mentioning a domain — which assert no-change behavior). Full suite: **1626 passed** (1610 + 16). `git diff --check` clean.

**Repair (`scripts/batch206_repair.py`, batch-200 gate + a batch-206 furniture rule — a dropped para is allowed when it is a bare-domain line or the tagline immediately before one in the stored text; `_FIGURE_RANGE_ROW_RE` added-block acceptance):** **8 repaired, 0 filled, 0 skipped** (re-run after `--write`: 0 would repair/fill). ## Batch 209 (2026-11-07): conference-call host notice + call-in-numbers break ends the guidance section; shared-line walk-back; 8 stored rows repaired

**Fix (`src/tickerlens/data/filings.py`, `_CALL_HOST_NOTICE_RE` + `_CALL_IN_NUMBERS_RE` + `_notice_starts_sentence` / `_notice_sentence_start` + shared-line walk-back in `extract_management_guidance`):** the batch-208 carry-forward (CI 2025-03-31's stored guidance row carries the conference-call notice block) is closed. Cigna's outlook section is followed by its call notice in a shape none of the batch-193 tests match — "Management will be hosting a conference call to review ... results and discuss full year ... outlook beginning today at 8:30 a.m. ET." (CI x6) and ConocoPhillips' same shape with the other verb form, "ConocoPhillips will host a conference call today at noon Eastern time to discuss this announcement." (COP x2). Neither line carries "webcast"/"replay", so `_CONFERENCE_CALL_NOTICE_RE` never fires, and the dial-in block after it (call-in numbers, Live Call, Passcode, Replay, "dial in") was collected as guidance. "will host(ing) a conference call" is the discriminator: a guidance sentence points AT the call (DHI's "The Company plans to also provide guidance ... on its conference call today", x11 — kept; Biogen's FX line "... will be provided on the conference call and webcast" — kept), it never says the company will host one. "will host its calls" (GE 2023-12-31's earnings-call schedule line) never matches: no "a conference call". The guard is sentence-aware (`_notice_starts_sentence`, 120 chars from the sentence start — the batch-193 number, applied to the sentence instead of the line): CI 2024-09-30's notice shares its line with the preceding availability sentence, so the match starts ~180 chars into the line but 11 chars into its own sentence, and the 120-char line guard would have blocked it. **Walk-back:** the notice sentence may share its line with the tail of the preceding sentence, so breaking at the line leaves the kept text ending mid-sentence ("This quarterly earnings", "... Investor Relations section") — when the notice sentence does not start the break line and the text before it ends with sentence punctuation, unterminated kept lines are popped so the text ends at the last complete sentence (the heading is never popped; runs only on a batch-209 notice match).

**Tests:** 12 in `tests/data/test_filings_batch209.py` (real-exhibit pins for CI 2025-03-31, CI 2025-09-30 incl. the walk-back, and COP 2024-12-31; regex accept/reject sets; sentence-guard pins incl. the CI 2024-09-30 shared-line shape and the >120-char late-match rejection; guard pins for DHI's pointer and GE's schedule line, both kept). Pin is behavioral: on stashed old code the test file errors at collection (`_CALL_HOST_NOTICE_RE` does not exist). Full suite: **1664 passed** (1652 + 12). `git diff --check` clean.

**Blast (cache-only old-vs-new over 1,699 exhibits, `scripts/batch209_cache_blast.py`, `hidden_files/blast209/g_new` vs `blast208/g_new`):** **11 diffs, 0 None-flips — 8 pure tail cuts (CI x6 + COP x2) and 3 completions.** The 3 completions are the pre-seeding CI quarters (2023-12-31, 2024-03-31, 2024-06-30): the batch-204 lookahead keeps the freed budget lines once the notice break fires inside its window, so the guidance table completes and the disclaimer is served; they have no stored rows and serve on next refresh/seed. CI 2023-09-30 is byte-identical (its old extraction already ended before the notice). 1,688 byte-identical.

**Repair (`scripts/batch209_repair.py`, prefix-drop gate):** **8 repaired, 0 filled, 0 skipped** (re-run after `--write`: 0 would repair/fill). CI 2024-09-30 1865→1099, 2025-03-31 1827→1143, 2025-06-30 1876→1144, 2025-09-30 1858→1108, 2026-03-31 1916→1155, 2026-06-30 1889→1156; COP 2024-12-31 912→599, 2025-12-31 1045→732. ## Batch 210 (2026-11-07): table-of-contents anchor rejection in the guidance extractor; DLR over-collection closed; 8 stored rows repaired

**Fix (`src/tickerlens/data/filings.py`, `_is_toc_entry` + anchor-loop skip in `extract_management_guidance`):** the batch-209 carry-forward (DLR over-collection) is closed. Every DLR stored guidance row (8 quarters, ~748 chars each) was its financial supplement's table of contents: the extractor anchored on the contents-page entry for "2025 Outlook" (line 19 of the line stream, followed by the bare page number "10") and spent its whole line budget on TOC titles ("Consolidated Quarterly Statements of Operations", "Funds From Operations ...", ...). The real outlook section sits ~750 lines later ("Digital Realty raised its 2025 Core FFO per share outlook to $7.15 - $7.25 ...") and was never reached, because the function returns the first anchor whose collection clears 100 chars. A heading candidate is now a TOC entry when the next line is a bare page number AND the line after that is another title followed by another bare page number — two consecutive (title, page-number) pairs — and the anchor is skipped so scanning continues to the real heading. The two-pair requirement is the guard: a single bare number after a heading is often just a page footer — ELV's "Financial Guidance Summary" is followed by "6" and then "Elevance Health" (the next page's content), and SPG's "Guidance Reconciliation" by "43" and then "(1)" — neither is a contents page, and neither anchor may be skipped. Both shapes are pinned unchanged.

**Tests:** 13 in `tests/data/test_filings_batch210.py` (`_is_toc_entry` unit pins incl. two-pair match, ELV/SPG/two-numbers/short-tail rejections; synthetic end-to-end TOC skip, ELV page-footer anchor, SPG reconciliation anchor; real-exhibit pins for DLR 2025-06-30, 2024-06-30, 2026-09-30; guard pins for ELV 2023-12-31 and SPG 2025-06-30). Pin is behavioral: on stashed old code the test file errors at collection (`_is_toc_entry` does not exist). Full suite: **1677 passed** (1664 + 13). `git diff --check` clean.

**Blast (cache-only old-vs-new over 1,699 exhibits, `scripts/batch210_cache_blast.py`, `hidden_files/blast210/g_new` vs `g_old`):** **12 diffs, 0 None-flips — all DLR, all TOC dumps replaced by genuine Core FFO outlook tables.** A pre-blast survey found 18 first-anchors followed by a bare number, but only the 12 DLR exhibits carry the two-pair signature; ELV (page footer) and SPG (page number + footnote marker) are correctly excluded. 1,687 byte-identical.
